# Koei Tecmo KTGL Commonalities & Cross-Venture Optimizations (`AoC` + `AoI`)

Both **[AoC Native](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android)** (*Hyrule Warriors: Age of Calamity*, `01002B00111A2000`, update `v327680` / `1.3.0`) and **[AoI Native](../README.md)** (*Hyrule Warriors: Age of Imprisonment*, `04003F40232DE000`, update `v196608`) are built by Koei Tecmo (Omega Force) on successive generations of the same **KTGL** + **KIDS** engine stack. Insights and fixes uncovered on either title carry directly across both ports.

---

## 1. Verified Engine, Graphics, Audio & Save Commonalities

| Subsystem | Age of Calamity (`01002B00111A2000`, `1.3.0`) | Age of Imprisonment (`04003F40232DE000`, `v196608`) | Shared Port Implications |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Engine & Job Graph** | `KTGL For NX(a64)` + `KIDSTaskScheduler` / `KIDSResourceScheduler`, `NintendoSDK 9.6.0` | `KTGL For Ounce(a64)` + `KIDS`, `NintendoSDK 20.5.15` | Multi-clock pacing (`KIDSTask`, KTGL device, scene float-fps); heavy atomics (`25,976` `ldaxr` / `24,855` `stlxr` in AoC `main`) |
| **Graphics API & Shaders** | NVN `53.312` (`NEAR_IS_MINUS_W`), Maxwell shaders, `230` GLSLC hits in `main` + KTGL `G2S` containers (`53` harvested `V` rows) | NVN 2 (`Ounce`), Ampere `sm86` (`161` Ampere + `322` GLSLC hits in `main`), `101,860` catalogued shaders (`15,449` runtime-seen), `0` mesh/task shaders | Separable `nvnCommandBufferBindProgram` (`VS`/`FS`/`CS`), dedicated `c1` constant-buffer slices, large descriptor/texture pools |
| **VFS & Containers** | `1,101` RomFS files (`11.72 GB`): `8` `.rdb` (`_DRK0000`), `24` `.rdb.bin*` (`IDRK0000`), `970` `asset/data/*.file`, `97` `.webm` | `2,174` RomFS files (`46 GB` NUP v1 `PKG[1]`): `root.rdb`/`system.rdb` + `1,922` `.fdata` (`PDRK0000`/`IDRK0000`) under `b6e28c40/`, `129` `.bin` under `ebc8c781/` | Streamed container-backed assets (`NUP` zstd chunks / merged `romfs.bin`), hash/offset indexed without loose-file explosions |
| **Audio Middleware** | `KTSL2` (`KTSL2HLUpdate`, `KTSL2_Render`, `ASRS`) + `OpusDecoder`; `1.3.0` requires `4 MiB` shift (`15+142 -> 19+138 MiB`) from data to engine heap | `KTSL2` over `nn::audio::OpenAudioOut` (6 ch -> `48,000 Hz` stereo AAudio, `0` underruns after boot) | Fixed upfront audio engine/data heap partition; zero CRIWARE ADX/Sofdec dependencies |
| **Video Playback** | `subsdk0` `NintendoSDK_movie` VP9 + Opus (`.webm`), decoded to `R8` Y + `RG8` UV NV12 textures | `subsdk0` `NintendoSDK_movie` VP9 (`.bin` EBML `.webm`), `1920x1080` at `59.7–59.8 fps` (`8.17 ms/packet`) | Host `libvpx` (`4` threads) + ARM NEON `vst2q_u8` planar YUV-to-NV12 interleave ([`dm/host/hle_movie.c`](../dm/host/hle_movie.c)) |
| **Texture Pool Sub-Allocation** | Small/static CPU-mapped KTGL textures packed into shared `64 KiB` dirty-tracking units alongside per-frame CPU uniform/staging writes | Small `2x1` format `0x2e` textures packed on shared `4 KiB` pages alongside active CPU writes | Naive re-protection causes `SIGSEGV` + `mprotect` + `fault_run` (`g_frun = 256` pages) storms (`2 * 257 = 514` released pages/frame on AoI) |
| **Indirect `QUADS` Draws** | `nvnCommandBufferDrawArraysIndirect` with `prim == 7` (`QUADS`), GPU-written `16`-byte indirect buffers | `DrawArraysIndirect` with `prim == 7` (`QUADS`), GPU-written `16`-byte indirect buffers | Vulkan lacks `QUADS` topology; converted via the shared `287`-word `quad_indirect_spv[287]` compute shader + `uint16` `0,1,2,0,2,3` index buffer |
| **Save Data Format** | Single flat `1 MiB` (`1,048,576` B) binary `svdt` (`SaveManager.FLAT_SAVE`) | Single flat `8 MiB` (`8,388,608` B) binary `DATA.BIN` committed at autosave points | No directory-per-slot tree; single fixed-size binary blob initialized at first boot and updated via state machine |

---

## 2. Cross-Venture Optimizations Implemented

### Optimization 1 — AoI (`DM_VK_EDGE_UNPIN=1`): Shared Boundary Page Unpinning & Edge Polling

- **Root Cause (Observed Live on AYN Thor):** KTGL sub-allocates small textures onto shared `4 KiB` pages adjacent to active CPU writes. Each re-protection triggers a `SIGSEGV`, and `nvn_dirty.c` `fault_run()` (`g_frun = 256`) releases up to `257` contiguous `PG_PROT` pages per shared page (`2 * 257 = 514` faults/frame), forcing a full scan of all `757` live images on every submission.
- **Implementation:**
  - [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_edge_unpin.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_edge_unpin.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h), and [`dm/gfx/tests/t_edge_unpin.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_edge_unpin.c).
  - Splits each tracked texture address span into partial boundary ranges `r[0]`, `r[1]` and exclusive interior pages `[ia, ie)`.
  - After `VKA_EDGE_UNPIN_AFTER` (`2`) consecutive neighbor-only skips, registers the texture in compact side-list `g_edge_hot[256]`, leaves shared boundary pages writable, keeps `[ia, ie)` write-protected via `PG_PROT`, and polls `[r0]`/`[r1]` via 64-bit hash comparison on every submit — skipping `SIGSEGV`, `mprotect`, and `full_scan`.
  - During the 2-frame warmup on straddling multi-page textures, snapshots an interior hash (`<= 1 MiB`) so `fault_run` collateral page releases are distinguished from genuine interior writes.

### Optimization 2A — AoC (`!vk_off("edgeguard")`): Pool-Aligned `64 KiB` Edge Hash Guard & Unpinning

- **Gap Addressed in AoC:** On Android/Linux, `nvn_dirty.c` protects memory in **`64 KiB` (`16`-page) units** (`g_unit`), and [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c) lacked sub-range edge hashing. Any small static KTGL texture sharing a `64 KiB` unit with per-frame CPU writes suffered both a fault storm and a **full Vulkan texture deswizzle + staging copy + 2 `vk_barrier_all` barriers** on every submission.
- **Implementation:**
  - [`nvn_dirty_unit_info`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty.c) + [`vk_edge_guard.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_edge_guard.h) + `vk_res.c` + `test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`.
  - `nvn_dirty_unit_info()` exposes the exact pool-aligned `64 KiB` unit base so boundary splitting aligns with `mprotect` granularity.
  - Snapshots head/tail sub-unit edge hashes (plus warmup interior collateral hash), unpins shared boundary units after `VK_EDGE_UNPIN_AFTER` (`2`) neighbor-only skips, and protects only exclusive full interior `[ia, ie)` units.

### Optimization 2B — AoC (`!vk_off("quadbatch")`): Pre-Pass Indirect `QUADS` Compute Batching

- **Gap Ported from AoI:** Converting GPU-pending indirect `QUADS` draws inline inside `draw_indirect` calls `vk_end_rendering_at(VK_PASS_END_COMPUTE, 0)` on each draw, splitting Adreno TBDR render passes per indirect quad draw.
- **Implementation (ported from AoI [`vka_exec.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c) / [`vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c)):**
  - [`vk_quad_batch.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_quad_batch.h) + [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c) + [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c).
  - `vk_qi_scan_ops()` walks upcoming opcodes (`OP_CALL` depth `16`, bounded at `64` items / `4,096` ops) before `begin_rendering_inner` opens the render pass (and on cache misses in `draw_indirect`), stopping cleanly on any buffer-write hazard (`OP_COPY_B2B`, `OP_UPDATE_UB`, `OP_DISPATCH`, `OP_SIGNAL_EVENT`, `OP_REPORT_COUNTER`, non-primitive `OP_BARRIER`) or target change (`OP_SET_RT`).
  - `quad_indirect_convert_batch()` converts up to `64` unique indirect `QUADS` buffers in one compute dispatch pass with a single `vkUpdateDescriptorSets` call and caches results keyed by `(submit_serial, vk_gpu_write_gen)`.


### Optimization 3 — AoI (`DM_MOVIE_LOOP_OPT=1`, `DM_MOVIE_FAST_NV12=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_MOVIE_MT=1`, `TOTK_MOVIE_FAST_NV12=1`): VP9 Row-MT / Loop-Filter Multithreading & Border-Only NV12 Packing

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoC (`movie_host.c`)** configured `libvpx` VP9 decoders with `threads = 4` in both `h_configure` and `h_flush` without enabling `VP9D_SET_ROW_MT` or `VP9D_SET_LOOP_FILTER_OPT`, leaving worker threads underutilized on 1–2 tile KTGL `.webm` cutscenes. It also copied Y planes row-by-row even when `stride == width`.
  - **AoI (`hle_movie.c`)** enabled `VP9D_SET_ROW_MT` without `VP9D_SET_LOOP_FILTER_OPT`, copied tight `stride == width` Y planes row-by-row, and pre-`memset` the entire `w * h * 1.5` (`3.1 MiB` at `1080p`) output buffer whenever `cw < w || ch < h` before overwriting the active rectangle.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/hle_movie_nv12.h`](../dm/host/hle_movie_nv12.h), [`dm/host/hle_movie.c`](../dm/host/hle_movie.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_movie_vpx.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_movie_vpx.c) — adds `VP9D_SET_LOOP_FILTER_OPT=1` (`DM_MOVIE_LOOP_OPT=1`), single contiguous Y `memcpy` when `cw == w && y_stride == w`, and border-only right/bottom `16`/`128` padding (`DM_MOVIE_FAST_NV12=1`).
  - **AoC:** [`movie_host.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/movie_host.c) + [`movie_nv12.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/movie_nv12.h) — calls `configure_vp9_mt()` (`VP9D_SET_ROW_MT=1` + `VP9D_SET_LOOP_FILTER_OPT=1`) in both `h_configure` and `h_flush` (`TOTK_MOVIE_MT=1`), plus `totk_movie_to_nv12()` contiguous Y copy & NEON `vst2q_u8` (`TOTK_MOVIE_FAST_NV12=1`).
- **Observed Nuances (Scientific Check):** On Apple Silicon arm64 (`libvpx` `1080p`, `4` threads), `ROW_MT + LOOP_FILTER_OPT` yields **`1.27x–1.28x`** on 1–2 tile streams over AoC's prior `row0/opt0` baseline (`7.666 -> 6.028 ms` on 1 tile, `4.429 -> 3.467 ms` on 2 tiles), while `LOOP_FILTER_OPT` alone adds `~1.03x–1.04x` over `ROW_MT` and is flat (`2.746 -> 2.749 ms`) when 4 tiles already saturate 4 threads. For `md_nv12`, eliminating the `3.1 MiB` full-frame `memset` yields **`1.25x–1.38x`** on `720p -> 1080p` border-padded frames, whereas already-contiguous `1080p` is DRAM-bandwidth-bound (`1.02x–1.04x`).

### Optimization 4 — AoI (`DM_VK_KTGL_CACHE=1`) + AoC (`!vk_off("ktglmemo")`): Zero-Write `512x4` Separable Program & Layout Front-Caches + Narrow `QUADS` Barriers

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:** KTGL binds separable per-stage `NVNprogram` objects (`VS`, `FS`, `CS`) across `350–1,000+` draws/frame. Direct-mapped `64`-slot program caches (`g_pm[64]` in AoI `vka_draw.c`, `g_progc[64]` modulo-`64` in AoC `vk_shader.c`) and `1`-entry layout memos (`lsh` in AoI, `last_layout` in AoC `vk_draw.c`) thrashed on alternating KTGL shaders, taking `pthread_mutex_lock` + FNV/`nvn_hm_get` slow paths against background PSO workers. In addition, indirect `QUADS` conversion emitted `3` global `ALL_COMMANDS` barriers per batch.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ktgl_cache.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ktgl_cache.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_ktgl_cache.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_ktgl_cache.c) — zero-write `4`-way set-associative `512x4` (`2,048`-entry) program cache (`vka_ktgl_pm_cache`, preserving `VKA_BAD_MISSING` `vka_layer_gen()` invalidation), `512x4` (`2,048`-entry) SplitMix64 graphics layout cache (`vka_ktgl_lay_cache`), `32x4` (`128`-entry) compute layout cache (`vka_ktgl_cs_cache`), plus narrow `TRANSFER -> COMPUTE_SHADER` and `COMPUTE_SHADER -> DRAW_INDIRECT` barriers in `qdx_convert()`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_ktgl_memo.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_ktgl_memo.h), [`vk_shader.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c) — zero-write `512x4` (`2,048`-entry) `vk_progc_memo` (with non-mutating `vk_progc_peek` for `vk_prog_predict`), `512x4` (`2,048`-entry) SplitMix64 `vk_lay_memo`, widened `pipe_get` `memo[256]`, narrow `VkMemoryBarrier2` barriers in `quad_indirect_convert_batch` & `quad_indirect_convert`, and fixed pre-existing `GAME_HWAOC` guard assertion in `tests/test_companion_state.py`.

### Optimization 5 — AoI (`DM_VK_PSO_MEMO=1`, `DM_FS_FAST_IMG=1`) + AoC (`!vk_off("ktglmemo")`, `TOTK_ROMFS_FAST=1`): Multi-Way PSO Front-Memos & Lock-Free Read-Side RomFS/NUP Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Graphics/Compute PSO lookup lock contention:** Even after caching separable programs and pipeline layouts (Opt 4), KTGL draws/dispatches still took a global PSO table mutex (`g_mu` in AoI `vka_pso.c`, `mu` + `nvn_hm_get` in AoC `vk_draw.c` / `vk_compute.c`) to look up compiled `VkPipeline` handles (`64`-slot direct-mapped cache in AoC thrashed across ~320 pipelines; AoI had no lock-free PSO front-cache and `cs_obtain` missed release barriers on async hit publication).
  - **Read-side VFS lock contention:** Both titles issue frequent read/stat calls against static compressed/packed archives (`NUP` zstd + RomFS mounts in AoI; `runtime_romfs_read`/`size` + `65,536`-slot handle table in AoC) while holding a coarse global lock on every lookup/read.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso_cache.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso_cache.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_pso_cache.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_pso_cache.c), [`dm/host/nup_image.h`](../dm/host/nup_image.h), [`dm/host/hle_fs.c`](../dm/host/hle_fs.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_nup_fast.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_nup_fast.c) — adds zero-RSS static `.bss` `512x4` dynamic-VIN (`<= 320` B) + `64x4` static-VIN (`<= 1,152` B) + `128x4` CS zero-write PSO front-caches (`DM_VK_PSO_MEMO=1`, preserving GPL `linked -> optimized` upgrades), release-published async `cs_obtain` hits, O(1) intrusive doubly-linked LRU eviction in `nup_z_trim`, lock-free CAS `nup_z_pin_read`/`nup_z_unpin_read` when `OpenFile` holds `refs >= 1`, and lock-free `img_path_fast` (`DM_FS_FAST_IMG=1`) for read-only non-overlay image mounts.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_ktgl_memo.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_ktgl_memo.h), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`vk_compute.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_compute.c), [`romfs_handles.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/romfs_handles.h), [`romfs.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/romfs.c) — upgrades `vk_pipe_memo` to O(1) SplitMix64 `1024x4` (`4,096` slots), adds `64x4` `vk_cs_memo` with release-published `e->state` and single-lock cold `vk_compute_pipeline`, and adds C11 seqlock handle resolution + O(1) FIFO `free_ring` (`TOTK_ROMFS_FAST=1`) with zero `GAME_*` branches.

### Optimization 6 — AoI (`DM_AUDIO_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_AUDIO_FAST=1`): KTSL2 Audio Mixing, Single-Pass ADPCM Resampling & Callback Ring Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoC (`audio_mixer.c`)** decoded each active mono/stereo `FMT_PCM16` voice via per-sample `Walk` struct copies + `walk_step()`/`sample_at()`, decoded `FMT_ADPCM` twice per sample (`s0` and `s1` lookahead), processed started zero-volume voices and zero-gain submixes through full channel-mix loops (`accumulate()`), ran `6 -> 2` sink downmix with per-sample channel-presence branches inside `frames`, and copied ring buffers sample-by-sample with `% RING_FRAMES` in `output_push()` / `pull_frames()`.
  - **AoI (`hle_audren.c`, `hle_audio.c`)** materialized an intermediate `int32_t st[FRAME_MAX * 2]` downmix buffer at `48 kHz` before clamping and computing `loud`/`peak`, and ran scalar loops for `6 -> 2` `int16_t` `AudioOut` downmix (`pull_device_x`) and PCM16 `SetAudioOutVolume` scaling (`pull_x`).
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/hle_audio_fast.h`](../dm/host/hle_audio_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_audren.c`](../dm/host/hle_audren.c), [`dm/host/hle_audio.c`](../dm/host/hle_audio.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_audio_fast.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_audio_fast.c) (`DM_AUDIO_FAST=1`) — fuses `48 kHz` `6->2` Q15 downmix + clamp + `loud` + `peak` reduction in `int64x2_t` NEON (`vmull_n_s32`/`vshrn_n_s64`, computing `vabsq_s32` peak before `int16_t` narrowing so `-32768` yields `+32768`), vectorizes `32 kHz` clamp+peak (`dm_audren_clamp_peak`), vectorizes `AudioOut` `6->2` `int16_t` Q15 downmix in `int64_t` (avoiding `int32_t` overflow on all-`-32768` inputs), and vectorizes PCM16 float volume scaling (`vcvtq_s32_f32`).
  - **AoC:** [`audio_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/audio_fast.h), [`audio_mixer.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/audio_mixer.c), [`test_audio_fast.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/test_audio_fast.c) (`TOTK_AUDIO_FAST=1`, `0` `GAME_*` branches) — uses 1–2 contiguous `memcpy` spans across `RING_FRAMES` wrap + IEEE-exact `0x7fffffff` `-0.0f`-safe NEON nonzero stereo scan, prunes non-ADPCM/non-biquad silent voices & zero-gain submixes cleanly, adds NEON/unit-step `FMT_PCM16` + single-pass `FMT_ADPCM` decoding (restoring `v->adpcm` before the final `+1` interpolation lookahead), NEON `vfmaq_n_f32` mix accumulation, and hoisted `6 -> 2` / `2 -> 2` sink downmix.

### Optimization 7 — AoI (`DM_VK_LC_FAST=1`, `DM_OPUS_FAST=1`) + AoC (`!vk_off("ktglmemo")`, `TOTK_OPUS_FAST=1`): Set-Associative GPU VA / Interval-Exact Lag Caches, Compute Driver-UBO Memoization & Lock-Free Hardware Opus Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **GPU VA -> VkMemory/Buffer & Lag Bound Lookups:** Even after 1-entry MRU front caches, high-churn texture/buffer draws and `vk_lag_bound` sweeps spilled to binary search + `pthread_rwlock_rdlock` (`vka_mem.c` `lookup()` in AoI; `nvn_vk.c` `span_best_fast()` over `64` hints, `resolve_local()`, `record_resolve_inner()`, and full linear scan under `lag_va_lock` in AoC). In addition, AoI `vka_draw.c` skipped compute driver-UBO memoization (`g_dm[5]`) completely.
  - **Hardware Opus Decoding & Frame Peak Scans:** AoC `opus_codec.c` serialized every `512`-slot hardware Opus decode under a single global `mu` mutex (`g_decoders` linear walk + `opus_decoder_ctl(OPUS_GET_NB_CHANNELS)`). AoI `hle_codec.c` resolved `objmap` handles under a global mutex (`GetOpusDecoderChannelCount` / `g_mdec_lock` list walk), freed request structs via heap `free`/`malloc` churn, and ran a scalar PCM16 peak scan (`-32768 -> +32768`).
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem_lc.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem_lc.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_vka_lc.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_vka_lc.c), [`dm/host/hle_codec_fast.h`](../dm/host/hle_codec_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_codec.c`](../dm/host/hle_codec.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_opus_fast.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_opus_fast.c) (`DM_VK_LC_FAST=1`, `DM_OPUS_FAST=1`) — adds read-only-on-hit `vka_lc4` (`1`-entry MRU + `256x4` `1 MiB`-bucket segment cache with lazy `set_gen` invalidation), tracks compute stage-5 UBO dirty generation (`s_ubo5`) + `k[4]` memoization in `g_dm` (memoizing direct compute `c0`/`c2`/`push` and indirect compute `c2`/`push`), adds `2x`-unrolled `vminq_s16`/`vmaxq_s16` NEON peak reduction (`-32768 -> +32768`), C11 seqlock `16x4` `dm_opus_cache` (`g_opus_c_cache` + `g_opus_mgr_cache`), cached `_Atomic(void *)` `opus_fn` pointers, and bounded `m->free_req` pooling.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_ktgl_memo.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_ktgl_memo.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w5.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w5.h), [`opus_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/opus_fast.h), [`opus_codec.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/opus_codec.c), [`test_opus_fast.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/test_opus_fast.c) (`TOTK_OPUS_FAST=1`, `0` new `#ifdef GAME_*`) — adds `vk_lag_iv_cache` (`4`-MRU + `128x2` interval-exact VA range cache avoiding `lag_va_lock`), `vk_ktgl_hint_set4` (`16x4` `1 MiB` pure read-only-on-hit hint front-cache over `hints[64]`, `resolve_local`, `record_resolve_inner`), and `64`-bucket sharded `g_buckets` (strict `bucket -> d->use_lock -> stats_lock` lock order) + `64x4` C11 seqlock format cache for `libopus` decoders.

### Optimization 8 — AoI (`DM_DESWIZZLE_FAST=1`, `DM_DIRTY_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_DESWIZZLE_FAST=1`, `TOTK_DIRTY_FAST=1`): Full-GOB Block-Linear Texture Swizzle/Deswizzle & Interval-Indexed / 64-Page Hierarchical Dirty Tracking

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Per-Segment Block-Linear Texture Copies (`nvn_deswizzle.c`):** Both AoI and AoC decoded and encoded `64 B x 8` (`512 B`) Tegra X1 / Switch 2 GOBs row-by-row with `16 B` index arithmetic per segment (plus per-GOB integer `/` and `%` mixed-radix coordinate decomposition in AoC `move()` and per-row `nvn_bl_offset()` in `move_region()`).
  - **Linear Live-Pool & Per-Page Dirty-Generation Scans (`nvn_dirty.c`):** AoI scanned `0..g_nr` live pools linearly inside `find_span()`, `on_fault()`, and `prep_write()` (`5,000+` pools) and scanned every `r->gen[i]` page individually in `nvn_dirty_since_span()` / `nvn_dirty_bounds_since()`, even when a single neighbor write in a shared pool pushed `r->latest` past `stamp`. AoC likewise walked every `4 KiB`/`16 KiB` page individually inside `nvn_dirty_since()` once `r->latest > stamp`, and performed duplicate `nvn_dirty_tracked()` + `find()` lookups in `nvn_dirty_wb_watch()`, `nvn_dirty_unit_info()`, and `nvn_dirty_lag_bounds()`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle.c`](../dm/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle.c), [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty.c`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_dirty_deswz_w6.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_dirty_deswz_w6.c) (`DM_DESWIZZLE_FAST=1`, `DM_DIRTY_FAST=1`) — adds full-GOB `4`-row-pair Tegra X1 (`deswz_gob_x1`) and `2x4`-row Switch 2 (`deswz_gob_sw2`) kernels over interior `64 B`-wide strips (`move_gob_strip`) while keeping partial edges/subranges exact; adds lock-free double-buffered `16,384`-entry Augmented Interval Index (`dirty_rix_insert`/`remove`/`collect` with prefix `max_end` pruning for overlapping `4 KiB`-aligned pools sharing `16 KiB` host pages) across `find_span`/`on_fault`/`prep_write`, plus `64`-page `cmax[]` superpage summaries and `r->latest` early-out guarded by `r->unprot` (`GEN_UNPROT` safety).
  - **AoC:** [`nvn_deswizzle_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle_fast.h), [`nvn_deswizzle.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/tex/nvn_deswizzle.c), [`nvn_dirty.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_dirty.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w6.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w6.h) (`TOTK_DESWIZZLE_FAST=1`, `TOTK_DIRTY_FAST=1`, `0` new `#ifdef GAME_*`) — replaces per-GOB `/` and `%` with a division-free mixed-radix odometer (`totk_deswz_move_fast`), unrolls interior GOBs (`totk_gob_x1_full`) and LUT-indexed partial boundary GOBs (`totk_gob_x1_edge`), hoists `move_region` block/slice strides with `64 B`/`16 B` aligned unrolling (`totk_deswz_region_bl`), maintains monotonic `64`-page `cmax[]` chunks in `vma_unprotect_unit`/`unprotect_unit` for `nvn_dirty_since()`, and deduplicates double pool lookups.

### Optimization 9 — AoI (`DM_VK_TEX_FAST=1`, `DM_SYNC_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_RES_FAST=1`, `TOTK_SYNC_FAST=1`): Read-Clean Texture/Sampler Front-Memos & Victim Caches and `O(1)` Horizon Kernel Synchronization

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Direct-Mapped Texture/Sampler Cache Thrash (`vka_tex.c`, `vk_res.c`):** AoI used 1-way power-of-2 modulo front-memos (`g_vm[2048]`, `g_sm[512]`, `m[2048]`) where power-of-2 ID strides caused 100% conflict misses. AoC used direct-mapped `T_texmemo[2048]` (`key >> 11` colliding inside `wide=2048` texture draws) and `T_tidc[4096]` without a collision victim cache; additionally, real `vktest --texture-cache-bench` proved that shrinking primary sets (`4096 -> 1024`) or swapping MRU entries on read hits causes L1/L2 dirty-line thrashing (`659 -> 2323 ns/draw`), whereas preserving `>= legacy` spatial bijection with read-clean hits plus compact `256x4` victim caches eliminates conflict misses without hit regression.
  - **Linear Horizon Kernel Scans (`hle_thread.c`, `threads.c`):** Both emulators share the `4096`-slot Horizon handle table (`h_new` scanning linearly from slot 1), flat `g_wait` linked list across lock/condvar/addr/sync waiters (`pick`, `wait_unlink`, `lock_handoff`, `0x1D`, `0x35`), unconditional `wake_sync_waiters` walks across `WaitSynchronization` sleepers, and linear `g_se[512]` `SystemEvent` binding scans.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_tex_memo_w7.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_tex_memo_w7.c), [`dm/host/hle_sync_fast.h`](../dm/host/hle_sync_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_thread.c`](../dm/host/hle_thread.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_sync_fast.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_sync_fast.c) (`DM_VK_TEX_FAST=1`, `DM_SYNC_FAST=1`) — adds spatial-locality-preserving read-clean `2048x4`/`512x4`/`2048x4` front-memos (`vka_vmemo4`/`vka_smemo4`/`vka_cmemo4`); adds `O(1)` 64-word `MAX_H=4096` handle bitmap (`hle_hmap` with self-healing resync for white-box tests), 64-bucket doubly-linked wait queues (`g_wbucket`, `g_wsync`), 64-bit sync-object Bloom filter (`wbloom`), and `64x4` `SystemEvent` front-index (`hle_se_idx`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_res_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w7.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w7.h), [`sync_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/sync_fast.h), [`threads.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/threads.c), [`test_sync_fast.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/test_sync_fast.c) (`TOTK_VK_RES_FAST=1`, `TOTK_SYNC_FAST=1`, `0` new `#ifdef GAME_*`) — upgrades `vk_tex_by_id` to `4096 + 256x4` two-tier read-clean `totk_fmemo`, backs `T_tidc[4096]` with `256x4` `totk_tidc_vic` on misses, adds `512x4` read-clean `totk_scache4`, promotes `view_get` only on `i >= 2`, and mirrors the full `O(1)` Horizon sync fast path (`sync_hmap`, `g_wbucket`/`g_wsync`, `wbloom`, `sync_se_idx`).

### Optimization 10 — AoI (`DM_VK_SHADER_FAST=1`, `DM_VK_DSET_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_SHADER_FAST=1`, `TOTK_VK_DSC_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Read-Clean Shader Specialization Memos & Multi-Material Driver-Block / Descriptor-Set Reuse

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Shader Specialization Mutex & Linear Link Scans (`vka_shader.c`, `vk_shader.c`, `vk_record.c`):** Repeating multi-material draw sequences resolved varying/alpha/GS-link/image-view/tex-view SPIR-V specializations, pipeline layouts, and descriptor-update templates through global mutexes (`g_spec_mu`, `g_link_mu`, `g_vfix_mu`) or hash-map walks on every draw, plus linear `strchr` token copies in AoC `deps_match()` and repeated `vka_shader_pfb_pick()`/`vka_shader_make()` linear scans in AoI.
  - **1-Slot Driver-Block Thrash & Direct-Mapped Descriptor Evictions (`vka_draw.c`, `vk_draw.c`):** Alternating across `>= 2` active materials thrashed `1`-entry driver-block memos (`g_dm[]` / `last[]` / `drv_seg()`), triggering repeated `2.5 KiB` CPU block regeneration (`vka_drv_fill` / `driver_bank_fill`), full byte compares, and transient arena copies per draw. In descriptor-set resolution, AoC used direct-mapped `g_dsc[2048]` without a collision victim cache, and AoI `sets_push()` lacked a front semantic/write memo (`vka_dset_memo4`) and redundant bind elision (`g_bound_set0`).
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shader_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shader_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dset_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dset_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shader.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shader.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_draw.inc`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_draw.inc), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_shader_dset_w8.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_shader_dset_w8.c) (`DM_VK_SHADER_FAST=1`, `DM_VK_DSET_FAST=1`) — adds per-thread read-clean `256x4` (`vka_sh_pair64_memo4`, `vka_sh_tex_memo4`) and generation-stamped `512x4` `vka_pfb_memo4` front-caches, `64x4` semantic-key `vka_drv_key4` queried before `vka_drv_fill()` with `64x4` `vka_drv_ring4` content fallback, `16x4` `vka_aref_memo4`, `512x4` `vka_dset_memo4`, and `g_bound_set0[2]` bind elision.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_shader_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader_fast.h), [`vk_dsc_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_dsc_fast.h), [`vk_int.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_int.h), [`vk_shader.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader.c), [`vk_record.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_record.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w8.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w8.h) (`TOTK_VK_SHADER_FAST=1`, `TOTK_VK_DSC_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*`) — adds `_Thread_local` `256x4` `totk_vfix_memo4` with 64-bit packed `kind_pack`, epoch-stamped `alpha_memo[8]`, `512x4` `totk_lay_memo4` / `totk_tmpl_memo4`, zero-copy `totk_deps_next`, `64x4` semantic-key `totk_gen_memo4` before `driver_bank_fill()`/`support_fill()`, `64x4` `totk_blk_memo4` content ring, and `256x4` exclusive descriptor-set victim cache `totk_dsc_vic`.

### Optimization 11 — AoI (`DM_VK_VIN_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_VB_FILTER=1`): Multi-Material Vertex-Input / Placed-Segment Front-Memos & Command-Buffer Vertex/Index/Zero-VBO Bind Filtering

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Vertex-Input Rebuilding & Segment Lookup Thrash (`vka_draw.c`, `vk_draw.c`):** Alternating across `>= 2` materials thrashed AoI's 1-slot vertex-input cache (`VI.v_gen` / `v_ctrl_hash`), forcing repeated attr/stream scans, ~1.1 KiB `VkVertexInputAttributeDescription2EXT` + `VkVertexInputBindingDescription2EXT` rebuilds, and repeated `vkCmdBindVertexBuffers2EXT` + `vkCmdSetVertexInputEXT` calls plus per-buffer `vka_addr_run()` lookups in `ubo_desc()`, `ssbo_desc()`, and `vin_bind()`.
  - **Per-Draw Vertex/Index/Zero-VBO Bind Storms & Recorder Scalar Hashing (`vk_draw.c`, `vk_record.c`):** AoC's `draw_buffers()` resolved each stream via `vk_resolve_for()`, allocated and zeroed a transient `64 B` dummy VBO via `vk_transient()`, and emitted individual `vkCmdBindVertexBuffers2(..., 1, ...)` calls plus `vkCmdBindIndexBuffer()` per draw even when bindings across consecutive draws in the same command buffer/submission serial were unchanged or contiguous. Meanwhile `vk_record.c` `descriptor_set()` still used a scalar 8-byte loop to hash 512 B `VkDescriptorBufferInfo`/`VkDescriptorImageInfo` payloads instead of `vk_dsc_fast.h`'s dual-lane `totk_hash_dual64`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_vin_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_vin_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_vin_fast_w9.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_vin_fast_w9.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_VIN_FAST=1`) — adds L1-compact `16x4` (`64`-slot, 40 B/entry metadata) `vka_vinfast_vc_memo4` paired with `g_vc_vk[64]` zero-copy Vulkan descriptor storage (passing `g_vc_vk[slot].{b,a}` by pointer to `vkCmdSetVertexInputEXT` and syncing `VI.{b,a}` lazily via `vi_sync_from_slot()` only before instancing), `vka_vinfast_vbc` stream-subset tracker (resolving `vi_zb < 16` collisions against stream 15 and resetting cleanly at `vka_dyn_invalidate()` and both `istart`/`ijoin` bind sites), and generation-stamped `128x4` `vka_addrm4` placed-segment memo (`run == 0` never cached) in `addr_run_fast()`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_vb_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_vb_fast.h), [`vk_int.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_int.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`vk_record.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_record.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w9.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w9.h) (`TOTK_VK_VB_FILTER=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*`) — adds `totk_vb_state` tracking `(cb, sub)` across 17 vertex slots (`0..15` + `ZERO_BINDING`), index buffer state (`ib_u`, `ib_off`, `ib_type`), and per-submission zero VBO (`zero_sub`, `zero_buf_u`, `zero_off`), coalesces dirty contiguous vertex slots via `totk_vb_next_run()` into minimal `vkCmdBindVertexBuffers2()` runs in `draw_bind_vbufs()`, adds `64x4` `totk_vbuf_memo4` in `vbuf_resolve_fast()` keyed on `vk_span_gen()`, invalidates cleanly at `descriptor_unbind()` and present/post/slot resets, and switches `vk_record.c` `descriptor_set()` to `totk_hash_dual64()`.

### Optimization 12 — AoI (`DM_VK_WALK_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_STAGE_FAST=1`): Multi-Material Stage Resource-Walk / Hazard Summary Memos & Parallel Worker Descriptor-Slice Memos

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Per-Draw Stage Binding Walks & Secondary Resource Loops (`vka_draw.c`):** On every draw, `walk_all()` re-invoked `vka_stage_bind()` per active shader stage even when alternating across repeated materials (`sh->e`, `s_ssbo`, `s_tex`, masked `ubo`), and then `tex_masks()`, `make_images()`, `draw_writes()`, `bar_hazard()`, `depth_sampled()`, `ub_hazard()`, and `img_retype()` re-scanned `g_wk.r[st][0..g_wk.n[st]-1]` repeatedly when a stage had no matching resource class at all.
  - **1-Slot Stage Freeze Thrash & Uncached Worker Descriptor Slices (`vk_draw.c`, `vk_record.c`):** Alternating across `>= 2` shaders or materials thrashed `g_freeze_memo[128]`'s 1-entry-per-shader freeze cache inside `record_stage_impl()`, forcing redundant resource walks and `dsc_resolve()` calls, while `vk_record.c` `record_job()` re-ran `descriptor_set()` (lookup/alloc/`vkUpdateDescriptorSets`) on identical `{layout, update, first, count, lag_ticket}` slices within the same job, and `dsc_resolve()` hashed descriptor arrays with a scalar loop.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_walk_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_walk_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_walk_fast_w10.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_walk_fast_w10.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_WALK_FAST=1`) — adds L1-split `256x4` shader-trait memo `vka_walk_sh_memo4` (`vka_walk_shader_traits()`) and `64x4` (`256`-slot) L1-split metadata/slot memo `vka_walk_meta4` + `vka_walk_slot[256]` (`vka_walk_memo4_find()`, `vka_walk_memo4_put()`) in `walk_stage_one()`, plus `vka_walk_summarize()` `VKA_WF_*` stage resource summary bits (`g_wk.fl[st]`) enabling `O(1)` short-circuits in `tex_masks()`, `make_images()`, `draw_writes()`, `bar_hazard()`, `depth_sampled()`, `ub_hazard()`, and `img_retype()`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_stage_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_stage_fast.h), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`vk_record.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_record.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w10.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w10.h) (`TOTK_VK_STAGE_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*`) — adds L1-split `128x4` (`512`-slot) multi-material stage freeze memo `totk_stage_freeze4` + `totk_stage_freeze_slot[512]` (`record_stage_fast_hit()`, `record_stage_fast_put()`) in `record_stage_impl()` when `!stats` (`vk_xlate_draw_stats.h` untouched), switches `dsc_resolve()` to `totk_hash_dual64()`, and adds a per-job-worker `64x4` (`256`-slot) `totk_rec_slice_memo4` (`rec_descriptor_set_fast()`) in `record_job()` keyed by `slice_seq`.

### Optimization 13 — AoI (`DM_VK_STATE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_STATE_FAST=1`): Byte-Exact NVN State/Binding Deduplication, Dynamic-State Front-Memo & 8B/16B `nvn_hmap` Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **Redundant State-Stamp & `bind_gen` / `g_pipe_dirty` Thrash (`vka_draw.c`, `vk_draw.c`):** Pre-baked KTGL material display lists (`CallCommands`/`CopyCommands`) repeatedly re-bind identical `blend_st`, `color_st`, `chmask_st`, `ms_st`, `poly_st`, `ds_st`, `va_st`, `vs_st`, UBO/SSBO/texture/sampler/image handles, and viewport/scissor/push states before draws. AoI's `vka_state_op()` unconditionally bumped `d->bind_gen` and all domain stamps (`s_vfmt`, `s_vbuf`, `s_ubo5`, `s_ssbo`, `s_tex`, `s_push`, `ubo_dirty`), forcing downstream vertex-input/stage-walk/instancing invalidations; AoC's `vk_draw_exec()` unconditionally dirtied `g_pipe_dirty = 1` and re-ran `dynamic_state()` float viewport/scissor/stencil packing on every draw.
  - **Byte-Loop Hash/Compare Overhead in `nvn_hmap` (`nvn_hmap.h`):** Both backends store `8`-byte (`serial`/`layout`/`VkImage`) and `16`-byte (`program`/`layout`) keys heavily inside `nvn_hmap`, paying variable-length loop/runtime `memcmp` overhead on `nvn_hm_hash()`, `nvn_hm_get()`, `nvn_hm_put()`, and `nvn_hm_del()`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_hmap.h`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_hmap.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_state_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_state_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_state_fast_w11.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_state_fast_w11.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_STATE_FAST=1`) — adds logical-field and byte-exact state/binding deduplication (`vka_state_apply_*()`, preserving `g_vattr_clear` and `BindTexturesDeferred` `vka_addr`/`plat_mem_read` refresh before compare) in `vka_state_op()` + `vka_state_op_scalar()` so `bind_gen` and domain stamps only advance on real mutations, and specializes `nvn_hmap.h` (`klen == 8` & `16` unrolled word hash + word comparisons, `t_hmap` parity preserved).
  - **AoC:** [`nvn_hmap.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_hmap.h), [`vk_state_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_state_fast.h), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w11.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w11.h) (`TOTK_VK_STATE_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — gates `g_pipe_dirty`, `g_dyn_input_rev`, `OP_BIND_VATTR`/`VSTREAM`/`DEPTH_CLAMP`, and redundant `OP_BIND_TEX`/`SAMP`/`IMG` loops in `vk_draw_exec()`, adds revision-stamped `totk_dyn_memo` (`totk_dyn_memo_get()` / `totk_dyn_memo_put()`) around extracted `dynamic_state_pack()` in `dynamic_state()`, and adds identical `8`-byte and `16`-byte fast paths to AoC's `nvn_hmap.h`.

### Optimization 14 — AoI (`DM_VK_DYN_MEMO=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_CS_FAST=1`): Dynamic-State / Push-Constant Burst Elision (AoI) & Compute Program Memo / Pipeline Bind Elision (AoC)

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — Per-Draw Dynamic State & Push-Descriptor Re-Emission (`vka_draw.c`, `vka_exec.c`):** Even when `DM_VK_STATE_FAST` preserves `bind_gen` across consecutive draws inside a subpass, `vka_draw` unconditionally re-emitted dynamic pipeline state (viewport, scissor, depth bias, stencil, cull/front-face, primitive topology, blend constants) and scanned/allocated push-descriptor slots on every draw call.
  - **AoC — Repeated Compute Program Lookup & Redundant Compute Pipeline Binds (`vk_shader.c`, `vk_draw.c`):** Compute-heavy KTGL post-processing and extraction passes repeatedly resolved identical `(prog_u, sig5, serial)` compute programs through `vk_prog_resolve_compute()` and unconditionally re-bound `VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_COMPUTE` before `dispatch()`, even when the active compute pipeline, shader serials, and compute command buffer state had not changed.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dyn_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dyn_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_dyn_fast_w12.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_dyn_fast_w12.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_DYN_MEMO=1`) — adds per-command-buffer dynamic-state guard `vka_dyn_guard` (`vka_dyn_guard_hit()` / `vka_dyn_guard_put()`, invalidated via `vka_dyn_invalidate()` across subpass boundaries, `g_fs_sub || g_pp_side` prepasses, `g_rtl_pass`, and DLSS/FSR3 early returns in `vka_exec.c`) plus single-pass zero-allocation `push_same_fast()` and `vka_draw_sub_begin` caching of `g_push_dedup`, `g_push_delta`, and `g_xp_scissor`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_cs_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_cs_fast.h), [`vk_shader.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w12.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w12.h) (`TOTK_VK_CS_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — adds 4-way positive-only compute program memo `totk_cs_prog_memo4` indexed solely by `prog_u` (evicting stale `serial` slots) inside `vk_prog_resolve_compute()`, plus per-command-buffer `totk_cs_pipe_state` bind elision in `dispatch()` with explicit invalidation at descriptor unbinds, quad-indirect conversions, framegraph extraction hooks, and present/AHB/post boundaries.

### Optimization 15 — AoI (`DM_VK_CS_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_PROG_FAST=1`): Compute Dispatch Front-Memo, Pipeline Bind Elision & `vka_env_gen` Diag Cache (AoI) / Program Prepare & `(VS, FS)` Pair Front-Memos (AoC)

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — Per-Dispatch Resolution, Redundant Compute Pipeline Binds & `getenv()`/`snprintf()` Diag Polling (`vka_draw.c`, `vka_memu_draw.inc`):** Every compute dispatch repeated shader resolution, layout lookup, pipeline lookup, `vkCmdBindPipeline(VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_COMPUTE)` emission, and per-draw/per-dispatch `getenv()`/`strtoull()`/`snprintf()` parsing for `DM_VK_MESH_SKIP`, `DM_VK_BIND_LOG`, `DM_VK_DISPATCH_SKIP`, and `DM_VK_DISPATCH_SKIP_KEY`.
  - **AoC — Repeated Graphics Pair Resolution & Program Prepare Probing (`vk_shader.c`):** Two-stage `(VS, FS)` draws in `vk_prog_resolve_gfx()` / `vk_prog_resolve_gfx_record()` and `vk_prog_prepare()` walked `prog_of()` + per-stage mutex/hash lookups across consecutive identical material/epoch/signature draws.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_cs_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_cs_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_draw.inc`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_draw.inc), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_cs_fast_w13.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_cs_fast_w13.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_CS_FAST=1`) — adds 64x4 `vka_cs_disp_memo4` (keyed by `cprog_u`, `serial`, `layer_gen`, caching `sh[5]`, `vka_layout`, and non-null `VkPipeline`), per-CB compute pipeline bind elision `vka_cs_pipe_state` (invalidated in `vka_dyn_invalidate()`, `nf_pass()`, and `vka_memu_draw.inc`), and `vka_env_gen`-cached `vka_diag_keys` / `vka_diag_range` parsers.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_prog_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_prog_fast.h), [`vk_shader.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w13.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w13.h) (`TOTK_VK_PROG_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — adds positive `(prog_u, serial, epoch)` 64x4 memo `totk_prog_prep_memo4` in `vk_prog_prepare()`, and separable `(VS, FS)` 128x4 pair front-memos `totk_gfx_pair_memo4` (`g_gfx_pair_memo` in `vk_prog_resolve_gfx()` and `g_rec_pair_memo` with `prev_reg` / `VK_REC_M_SIGNATURE` parity in `vk_prog_resolve_gfx_record()`, guarded by `!progs[1] || out[1] != NULL`).

### Optimization 16 — AoI (`DM_VK_ENC_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_NVN_SUB_FAST=1`): SPSC Tail Shadow, Descriptor-Pool Seqlock & Packed UBO/SSBO Writers (AoI) / Deferred-Bind Span Slice, Per-Submit Handle Dedup & Zero-Memset Buffer Binds (AoC)

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — Acquire Tail Loads, Mutex-Serialized `dpool_base()` & Zero-Fill Bind Packing (`vka_enc.c`, `hle_nvn.c`):** `rec_alloc()` executed `atomic_load_explicit(&g_tail, memory_order_acquire)` on every command record even when producer headroom was already known, every deferred/sampler handle lookup locked `g_dpool_mu` inside `dpool_base()`, and every UBO/SSBO bind ran generic `REC()` `memset` before writing all struct bytes.
  - **AoC — Full-Copy Deferred Walks, Repeated Handle Visits & Generic `REC()` Zero-Fill (`hle_nvn.c`, `nvn_vk.c`):** `nvnQueueSubmitCommands()` and `ucap_build()` walked every record in every submitted command copy even when zero deferred binds were recorded or handles repeated within a submit, and buffer-bind recording zero-filled 24-byte payloads prior to full assignment.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_enc_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_enc_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_enc.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_enc.c), [`dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c`](../dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_enc_fast_w14.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_enc_fast_w14.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_ENC_FAST=1`) — adds producer-side `g_tail_shadow` (`vka_spsc_has_room`), seqlock-guarded `g_dpool_seq` + `vka_dpool_seq_find` lock-free pool lookup with mutex fallback, and dedicated packed UBO/SSBO writers (`vka_pack_ubo_small_one`/`_batch`, `vka_pack_buf_one`, `vka_pack_ssbo_batch`).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_nvn_sub_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/totk_nvn_sub_fast.h), [`hle_nvn.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w14.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w14.h) (`TOTK_NVN_SUB_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — records `[def_lo, def_hi)` deferred-bind byte spans in `copy_prefix` during `flatten_recording()` (`flatten_note_def` incl. raw `OP_CALL` & `call_marker_target` propagation) so `resolve_deferred_copy()` skips clean copies in `O(1)`, deduplicates submit/`ucap_build` handles with `O(1)` lazy-generation `totk_sub_seen4` (`totk_sub_seen4_reset`, bypassed when profiling is active), and packs `OP_BIND_UBO`/`OP_BIND_SSBO` via `totk_pack_bind_buf_rec`.

### Optimization 17 — AoI (`DM_VK_TBUF_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_TBUF_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Texel-Buffer `VkBufferView`, Sampled Buffer-Image & `VkFormat` Front-Memos (AoI) / TLS 64x4 Texel-Buffer `VkBufferView` Front-Memo (AoC)

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — Three-Lock Linear Scans & Driver Format Probes Per Buffer-Texture Binding (`vka_tbuf.c`, `vka_tex.c`):** Every `vka_tbuf_view()` locked `g_tmu` (`vka_tex_id_buffer`), `g_pl_mu` (`nvn_pool_live`), queried `vkGetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties()` into the driver, locked `g_mu`, and scanned up to 256 `g_v` entries; every sampled buffer-texture pre-pass/bind (`vka_tbuf_pending()` + `vka_tbuf_image_view()`) repeated `tb_of()` + `g_mu` + `g_i[0..256)` scans even when already refreshed in the current frame.
  - **AoC — Double Mutex Acquisitions & Lag Serialization on Texel-Buffer Lookups (`vk_res.c`):** `tbuf_by_id()` executed `vk_treg_get()`, locked `g_tex_mu` inside `vk_tex_get()`, and locked `g_tex_mu` a second time (holding it across `vk_resolve` and `vk_lag_read*` during lag capture), serializing `vk_record` worker threads.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tbuf_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tbuf_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tbuf.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tbuf.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka.h), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_tbuf_fast_w15.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_tbuf_fast_w15.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_TBUF_FAST=1`) — exposes lock-free `vka_tex_id_tver(id)`, adds single-owner replay front-memos (`vka_tbuf_view_memo4` 64x4, `vka_tbuf_img_memo4` 64x4 invalidated by `vka_tbuf_wrote` `g_timg_wgen`/frame/`g_i_evict`, and `vka_tbuf_fmt_memo` 16x4) with pre/post `tver`+`epoch` race checks and atomic per-slot LRU timestamps (`g_v_last`/`g_i_last`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_tbuf_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_tbuf_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w15.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w15.h) (`TOTK_VK_TBUF_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — adds per-thread read-clean 64x4 `totk_tbuf_memo4` keyed by full 20-bit `tid` and `totk_tbuf_epoch(treg_rev, ident_rev, span_gen, tbv_evict_gen)` (`tbuf_fast_try()`), running `vk_ub_touch` and `vk_gpu_write*`/`vk_lag_read*` unlocked on hit and blocking transient `frozen_hit` lag buffers from polluting the base-pool memo (`g_tbv_evict_gen` bumped in `retire_bview` and `vk_res_buffer_released`).

### Optimization 18 — AoI (`DM_VK_GW_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_GW_FAST=1`): Wait-Free GPU Write-Range Summaries, Word-Wide Fill Reconstruction & 4-Way Write/Uniform Memos

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — `g_tmu` Mutex + Linear `g_gw` Scans, Bytewise `GW_FILL` Loops & 1-Slot Deferred-UB Memo (`vka_tex.c`, `vka_draw.c`):** `vka_tex_gw_read()` unconditionally acquired `g_tmu` and scanned every pending `g_gw` entry even when no pending GPU write or opaque/copy hazard overlapped `[addr, addr + n)`, `gw_content()` scanned `g_gw` under `g_tmu` on every upload, `GW_FILL` filled shadow bytes one byte at a time, and `vka_draw.c` `UB` scanned `UB.e[0..UB.n)` on every buffer/vertex/index draw check while caching only a single `UB.m_addr` shadow UBO slice.
  - **AoC — `g_gw_mu` Mutex Acquisitions on Disjoint Pending Checks & 1-Way `seen[256]` / `memo[1024]` Conflict Thrashing (`nvn_vk.c`):** `gpu_write_inner()` deduplicated writable SSBOs through a direct-mapped `seen[256]` table (repeatedly invoking `lag_guard_mark`, `g_gw_mu`, and `gw_add()` when colliding buffers alternated), `lag_original_pending()` used a direct-mapped `memo[1024]`, and `vk_gpu_write_pending()`, `ucap_build()`, `vk_ub_replay_read()`, and `update_ub()` acquired `g_gw_mu` even when `g_ngw == 0` or `[lo, hi)` missed all pending GPU writes.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gw_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gw_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_gw_fast_w16.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_gw_fast_w16.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_GW_FAST=1`) — publishes a wait-free seqlock `vka_gw_summary` with all-write and hazard-only (`GW_OPAQUE`|`GW_COPY`) `[min_lo, max_hi)` bounds + `64 KiB` chunk bloom (`vka_gw_may_overlap`, `vka_gw_may_hazard`, `vka_gw_sum_hit_locked`), skips redundant `memmove` when repeating the newest `gw_add` slot, replaces bytewise `GW_FILL` loops with UB-clean 64-bit word-wide `vka_gw_fill_bytes`, and adds a `4 KiB` page bloom (`vka_ub_bloom_mask`) plus 16x4 `vka_ub_shadow_memo4` to `UB`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_gw_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_gw_fast.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w16.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w16.h) (`TOTK_VK_GW_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — upgrades `gpu_write_inner()` deduplication to 64x4 `totk_gw_seen4` with in-place `(a, n)` slot reuse on `spans`/donor changes, adds wait-free seqlock `totk_gw_summary` (`totk_gw_may_overlap` / `totk_gw_hit_locked`) updated across `gw_add`/`gw_prune`/`lag_test_gw_index`, guards `g_gw_mu` acquisitions in `gw_pending`, `lag_original_pending`, `vk_gpu_write_pending`, `ucap_build`, `vk_ub_replay_read`, and `update_ub`, and upgrades `lag_original_pending()` to 256x4 `totk_orig_pend_memo4`.

### Optimization 19 — AoI (`DM_VK_SCR_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_UB_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Replay Scratch TLAB, Power-of-Two Alignment & L1-Resident 4-Way Indirect-Draw / Uniform-Snapshot Memos

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — `g_scrmu` Per-Allocation Contention & Redundant Indirect-Draw Look-Ahead Scans (`vka_dev.c`, `vka_exec.c`):** `vka_scratch()` acquired `g_scrmu` and performed integer-division alignment on every transient UBO/vertex/copy slice even though only the single replay encoder allocates and all `VK_WHOLE_SIZE` alignments are powers of two; `vka_exec.c` executed two separate full-command-buffer linear scans (`mdx_find()` then `qdx_find()`) followed by per-draw `mdx_copy()`/`qdx_convert()` linear searches across `g_mdx`/`g_qdx` tables.
  - **AoC — Integer-Division Alignment, Linear `snap_range_updated` Scans & 1-Way Uniform Memo Thrashing (`nvn_vk.c`):** Arena/transient offset bump allocators used 64-bit integer division (`((u+a-1)/a)*a`) for power-of-two `align`, `snap_find()`/`snap_range_updated()` binary-searched and linearly scanned `g_ub`/`g_snap` on every draw/UBO check, and `vk_ub_neg`, `vk_ub_rename` (`NM=1024`), `ub_rename_slow` (`NC=256`), and lagged-pointer caching relied on direct-mapped 1-way arrays that thrashed on colliding dynamic uniform buffers.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_scr_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_scr_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_scr_fast_w17.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_scr_fast_w17.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_SCR_FAST=1`) — replaces division alignment with power-of-two `vka_scr_align_up()`, gives the replay encoder a lock-free `vka_scr_tlab` (`128 KiB` chunk carved under `g_scrmu`, `16 KiB` direct bump cap), fuses `mdx_find()` + `qdx_find()` into a single-pass command scan `ind_scan_collect_both()`, and caches indirect-draw look-ahead hits in an L1D-resident `64x4` (`14 KiB` combined) `vka_ind_idx4` front-memo.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_ub_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_ub_fast.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w17.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w17.h) (`TOTK_VK_UB_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — introduces power-of-two `totk_align_up_sz()`, a `4 KiB` snapshot update `totk_ub_page_bloom()`, `_Thread_local` `64x4` `totk_snap_memo4`, and L1D-resident 4-way clock-replacement memos (`64x4` `totk_ub_neg_memo4`, `64x4` `totk_ub_rename_memo4`, `64x4` `totk_ub_copy_memo4`, `32x4` `totk_ub_ptr_memo4`) in `nvn_vk.c`.

### Optimization 20 — AoI (`DM_VK_VIEW_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_VIEW_FAST=1`): Lock-Free `VkImageView` / Identity / Feedback-Cut Front-Memos, Render-Pass Memo & Active-Slice Framebuffer Hashing

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles:**
  - **AoI — `g_tmu`/`g_rpmu` Mutex Acquires, Pointer-Chasing View Lists & Full-Struct `456`-Byte Bytewise FNV-1a (`vka_tex.c`, `vka_draw.c`):** `vka_view()` unconditionally locked `g_tmu`, ran `img_lists()`, walked and mutated the `VIEWS(im)` linked list on every attachment bind, blit, FSR3, and present view lookup; `rp_obtain()` acquired `g_rpmu` and linearly `memcmp`ed `56`-byte `rpkey` entries; and `fb_obtain()` / `fb_hash()` hashed all `456` bytes of `fbkey` byte-by-byte (including `7`–`8` zero `48`-byte `fbatt` slots on typical `1`–`2` attachment passes) followed by full `456`-byte `memcmp`.
  - **AoC — `g_tex_mu` Locks & Linear `im->views` Scans Across `vk_tex_view`, Identity & Feedback-Cut Queries (`vk_res.c`):** `vk_tex_view()`, `native_view()`, and `scene_image()` acquired `g_tex_mu`, re-decoded format/bpb/swizzle/subresource predicates in `view_get()`, and walked `im->views`; `vk_tex_view_identity()` and `vk_xlate_texture_view_type()` locked `g_tex_mu` to scan `im->views` and `im->native->views` on every draw feedback check; and `vk_tex_view_levels()` (`TOTK_VK_FEEDBACK_CUT`) locked `g_tex_mu` for a nested `O(nviews^2)` scan.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_view_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_view_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_draw.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_view_fast_w18.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_view_fast_w18.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_VIEW_FAST=1`) — adds a `64x4` `_Thread_local vka_view_memo4` in `vka_tex.c` keyed on `(im, k0, k1, g_view_evict_gen)` bumped in `img_destroy()` and all four `im->views = NULL` swap sites, a `32x4` `_Thread_local vka_rp_memo4` in `rp_obtain()`, and `8`-byte word-wide active-slice `vka_fbkey_hash_fast()` / `vka_fbkey_eq_fast()` + `32x4` `vka_fb_memo4` cleared on `fb_grow()` in `vka_draw.c`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_view_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_view_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w18.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w18.h) (`TOTK_VK_VIEW_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — adds lazy TLS `64x4` `totk_view_memo4` (`vk_tex_view`, `scene_image`, `native_view`) with canonical `totk_view_pack_fields()` + `tex_view_meta_key()`, SoA-tagged `16x4` `totk_view_id_memo4` shared between `vk_tex_view_identity()` and `vk_xlate_texture_view_type()`, and `16x4` `totk_view_cut_memo4` in `vk_tex_view_levels()`, all versioned by `im->incarnation` and `g_tex_ident_rev` within `~27.7 KiB` combined L1D footprint.

### Optimization 21 — AoI (`DM_NVN_HLE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_NVN_HLE_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Shader-Pool / Live-Pool / Pin Front-Memos, Span Slicing & Interval Bloom Filters

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (`hle_nvn.c` Mutex & Range Scans):**
  - **AoI — Command-Buffer Recording Stalls on `g_pl_mu`, Shader-Pool Scans & Host-Segment Sweeps (`dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c`):** On every shader bind, inline command-record check, `pl_get()` pool lookup, and dirty-range invalidation, `hle_nvn.c` walked `g_shp_n` shader-pool intervals with seqlock retries, acquired `g_pl_mu` for live-pool lookup (also blocking direct command recording when `g_shp_n > 0`), and scanned every busy slot in `g_hostseg[]` even for disjoint address ranges.
  - **AoC — Lock Contention on `g_pin_mu`, Full-Copy `pin_mark_ops` & Control-Copy Array Scans (`d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c`):** `pin_tex()` and `pin_serial_has()` acquired `g_pin_mu` per texture bind/invalidate, `pin_mark_ops()` scanned full flattened command copies even when pinned-texture ops lived inside a narrow slice or were absent, and `nvn_host_ctl_inval()` linearly scanned `g_ncopies` control-copy ranges on disjoint invalidations.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_hle_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_hle_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c`](../dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_hle_fast_w19.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_hle_fast_w19.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_hid.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_hid.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_host_stubs.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_host_stubs.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_NVN_HLE_FAST=1`) — adds a `1`-MRU + `64`-slot direct-mapped TLS negative cache (`nvn_shp_neg_cache`) backed by a `256`-bit `16 MiB`-chunk `nvn_shp_summary` bloom filter, a `32x4` TLS live-pool memo (`nvn_plive_memo4` synchronized via `nvn_pl_sync`), and a `256`-bit `64 KiB`-chunk `nvn_hostseg_summary` interval bloom (`nvn_hostseg_may_overlap()`), plus CI fixes (`t_hid.c` `CTRL_ARG_SIZE`, `t_host_stubs.c` weak stubs, ASan ignorelist, Python/doc/lint baselines).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_nvn_hle_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/totk_nvn_hle_fast.h), [`hle_nvn.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w19.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w19.h) (`TOTK_NVN_HLE_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_*` in `games/`) — adds flattened-copy `[pin_lo, pin_hi]` slice tracking (`totk_pin_span`), a packed `48`-bit-epoch lazy TLS `128x4` memo + `256`-bit serial bloom (`totk_pin_memo4` / `totk_pin_sync`), and a counting `64`-page / `256`-bit interval bloom (`totk_ctl_copy_summary`) guarding `g_ncopies` scans.

### Optimization 22 — AoI (`DM_VK_REG_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_REG_WB_FAST=1`): Descriptor-Registry Unchanged-Write Elision, Lock-Free TLS `vk_treg_get` Front-Memo & Flushed / Settled-Writeback Interval Summaries

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Descriptor-Registry Churn & Linear Span Scans):**
  - **AoI — Redundant `treg`/`mtl_reg_samp` Rewrites & Mutex-Bound `fl`/`ft` Interval Checks (`vka_tex.c`, `hle_nvn.c`):** Re-registering an identical texture or sampler handle dirtied descriptor slots (`g_tdirty`/`g_sdirty`), bumped `g_tver`/`g_sver`, and forced redundant sampler/slot work, while `fl_add()`, `nvn_flushed()`, `ft_flush()`, `ft_del()`, `nvn_ft_written()`, `nvn_ft_tracked()`, and `nvn_ft_poll()` unconditionally acquired `g_fl_mu`/`g_ft_mu` and scanned disjoint ranges or absent serials. Separately, `dm/gfx/tests/t_tiled_resolve.m` still called removed `nvn_rec_linear`, breaking the macOS `Build check` job.
  - **AoC — Mutex-Serialized `vk_treg_get()` & Linear `g_wb_lookup` Scans (`games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c`):** Draw-path `vk_treg_get()` calls took `g_tmu` on every lookup, `wb_lookup` was scanned linearly on disjoint GPU tile/invalidate/landing ranges and when `g_wb_lookup_n == 0`, and `wb_invalidate_pending()` acquired `g_wbi_mu` even when empty.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_reg_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_reg_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c`](../dm/gfx/nvn/hle_nvn.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_reg_fl_fast_w20.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_reg_fl_fast_w20.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_tiled_resolve.m`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_tiled_resolve.m), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_REG_FAST=1`) — adds unchanged texture/sampler registration elision (`nvn_treg_same` / `nvn_sreg_same` with `g_sv[]` slot-readiness preservation and cached `g_sres[id]` lookup in `vka_tex_prepare`), seqlock-guarded interval/serial bloom summaries (`nvn_fl_summary`, `nvn_ft_summary`), and a 4-way TLS covered-span memo (`nvn_fl_cov_memo4`) for `fl_add`/`nvn_flushed` (plus null-pointer guard in `nvn_flushed` and updating `t_tiled_resolve.m` to pass `handle` directly to `mtl_exec`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_reg_wb_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_reg_wb_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w20.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w20.h) (`TOTK_VK_REG_WB_FAST=1`, `0` `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — adds a lock-free TLS `64x4` `vk_treg` memo (`totk_treg_memo4`) validated on write, `64 KiB` / `256`-bit settled-writeback and active-slot range/count summaries (`totk_wb_summary`), and lock-free `g_wbi_n` emptiness fast-path (`wb_invalidate_pending`).

### Optimization 23 — AoI (`DM_VK_SERIAL_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_SERIAL_FAST=1`): Lock-Free TLS Texture-Serial / GPU-Ownership / Format Front-Memos & Shared-Image Idle Sweep Summary

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex-Serialized Texture-Serial Lookups & Full-Table Idle Scans):**
  - **AoI — `g_tmu` Serial / Ownership / Format Locks (`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`):** `vka_tex_get()`, `mtl_tex_gpu_owned()`, and `vka_tex_id_vkfmt()` acquired `g_tmu` on every call to probe `g_smap` or `g_treg[]`, even when `g_smap`, `gpu_owned`, video epoch (`dm_codec_video_epoch()`), and `g_tver[id]` were unchanged. Separately, `dm/host/tests/t_android_layerreq.c` raced `ask()` against `unwritten()` without a mutex, and `DM_VK_REG_FAST` in `docs/advanced_settings.tsv` lacked a trailing tab column.
  - **AoC — `g_tex_mu` `tex_get_inner()` Lookups & `g_by_storage` Commit Sweeps (`games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c`):** `tex_get_inner()` locked `g_tex_mu` and probed `g_by_serial` on every resolve, even for non-mirrored / GPU-written textures or repeated mirrored accesses within the same submission, while `vk_res_sweep()` walked the full `g_by_storage` hash table on every commit even when no shared image had idled 600 submissions.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_serial_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_serial_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_serial_fast_w21.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_serial_fast_w21.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_android_layerreq.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_android_layerreq.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_SERIAL_FAST=1`) — adds lock-free TLS `64x4` `vka_smap_memo4` (keyed on `(serial, g_smap_gen, dm_codec_video_epoch())` with slow-path split `tex_get_create_locked`), `64x4` `vka_gpu_own_memo4` + `g_gpu_own_ever`/`g_gpu_own_gen` fast-zero guard (`mtl_tex_gpu_owned`), and `32x4` `vka_vkfmt_memo4` keyed on `g_tver[id]` (`vka_tex_id_vkfmt`); plus `g_unw_mu` synchronization in `t_android_layerreq.c` and canonical `advanced_switches.py` formatting.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_serial_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_serial_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w21.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w21.h) (`TOTK_VK_SERIAL_FAST=1`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — adds lock-free TLS `64x4` `totk_tex_serial_memo4` (valid across submissions when `!mirrored || gpu_written` or within `clean_sub == sub` otherwise, invalidated via `g_tex_serial_gen`) and a `(count, min_idle_sub)` `totk_idle_shared_summary` skipping empty `g_by_storage` sweeps.

### Optimization 24 — AoI (`DM_VK_IMG_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_IMG_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Fence Reads, 4-Way Texture Metadata / Storage-View Front-Memos & Steady-State GPU-Owned Write Elision

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex-Serialized Fence Polling, Storage/Metadata Re-Probes & Redundant GPU-Write Locking):**
  - **AoI — `g_fmu` Fence Locks, Storage-View/Metadata `g_tmu` Misses & Redundant `vka_img_written_at()` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c`, `vka_tex.c`):** `vka_fence_last()` and `vka_fence_next()` locked `g_fmu` on every recording draw; `vka_tex_id_view_rec(storage != 0)`, `vka_tex_id_pending_rec()`, `vka_tex_id_target()`, `vka_tex_id_buffer()`, and `vka_tex_id_storage_pending()` skipped `g_vm4` and locked `g_tmu`; and `vka_img_written_at()` acquired `g_tmu` even on steady-state images already owned on the GPU (`gpu_owned && !m.z && !edge_ix && !edge_unpinned && !wb`).
  - **AoC — `g_mu` Fence Reads, 1-Slot `tid` Cache Thrash, Clear-Pending Scan & Redundant `tex_mark_gpu_written()` (`games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c`, `vk_draw.c`, `vk_res.c`):** `mtl_last_fence()` / `vk_gpu_idle()` locked `g_mu` around `g_reserved_fence`; `tex_by_id_cached` used a 1-entry slot per `tid` and re-checked `nvn_dirty_sub()` under `g_tex_mu`; `OP_DRAW` scanned `clear_color[]` + `clear_depth`; and `img_gpu_written()` acquired `g_tex_mu` on steady unmirrored GPU-written textures.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_img_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_img_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/tests/t_img_fast_w22.c`](../dm/gfx/tests/t_img_fast_w22.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_IMG_FAST=1`, disable with `0`) — release/acquire atomic `g_fence` reads (`vka_fence_last()`/`vka_fence_next()`), TLS `32x4` `vka_sview_memo4` + `64x4` `vka_tmeta_memo4` keyed on `(g_tver[id], g_tgen, pfin)` (with pre-existing `g_tver[id] != 0` guard and double-checked `tmeta_key_same`), atomic state stores (`im_swap_mem`, `gpu_owned`, `edge_ix`, `edge_unpinned`, `last_fence`), and lock-free steady `vka_img_steady_gpu_owned` fast path.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_img_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_img_fast.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w22.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w22.h) (`TOTK_VK_IMG_FAST=1` / `TOTK_VK_OFF=imgfast`, `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — acquire-atomic `g_reserved_fence`, TLS `256x4` `totk_tidc4` validating `tex_cell`/`img_cell` stamps prior to `vk_tex *` dereference plus `totk_tidc4_needs_sub_check`, compacted `R.clear_pending_mask` + `32x4` `totk_clear_read_memo4`, and `totk_tex_steady_gpu_written` lock-free fast path.

### Optimization 25 — AoI (`DM_HID_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_HID_FAST=1`): Lock-Free HID Pad / Motion / IMU Seqlock Shadows, Six-Axis Broadcast & Silent/Unchanged Rumble Elision

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex-Serialized HID / Motion / IMU / Haptics Polling, Redundant Multi-Sample Shake Noise & Zero/Duplicate Rumble Dispatches):**
  - **AoI — `g_motion_live[p].mu`, `g_imu_lock`, `six_states` / `h_hid_ImuSamples()` Loops & `dm_haptics` (`dm/host/hle_hid.c`, `dm/host/hle_haptics.c`):** `motion_live()` took `live_motion.mu` on every 60/120 Hz query; `h_hid_ImuIs()` took `g_imu_lock`; `six_states()` / `h_hid_ImuSamples()` recomputed 16 per-sample noise iterations even outside shake windows; and `dm_haptics_send()` / `dm_haptics_enabled()` / `dm_haptics_available()` / `dm_haptics_actual()` acquired `g_hap_lock` even on unchanged rumble batches.
  - **AoC — `g_lock`, `g_motion_lock`, `g_vib_lock` & Silent Rumble Dispatches (`games/totk/runtime/input.c`, `frontend_android.c`, `motion_fusion.c`, `hid_feedback.c`):** `pad_states()`, `style()`, `disconnected_states()`, `get_hold_type()`, `touch_state_1()`, `mouse_states()`, and `keyboard_state()` acquired `g_lock` on every poll; `motion_fusion.c` acquired `g_motion_lock` even when idle or unchanged; and `hid_feedback.c` (`vib_send_values()` / `vib_actual()`) acquired `g_vib_lock` and dispatched consecutive zero-amplitude rumble frames to Java.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/hid_fast.h`](../dm/host/hid_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_hid.c`](../dm/host/hle_hid.c), [`dm/host/hle_haptics.c`](../dm/host/hle_haptics.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_hid_fast_w23.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_hid_fast_w23.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_HID_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — C11 `_Atomic`-word seqlocks (`hid_fast_motion_slot`, `hid_fast_hap_slot`, bounded `HID_FAST_RETRIES=8`), atomic `_Atomic int g_imu_on` / `g_hap_en_fast` / `g_hap_avail_fast` / `g_hap_active_fast` (keeping `available()` independent of `suspended`), non-shake single-sample broadcast in `six_states()` and `h_hid_ImuSamples()`, cached `dm_rumble_log_fast_on()`, hoisted `dm_haptics_available()` in `vib_send()`, and memberwise `hid_fast_vib_eq()` unchanged-batch lock elision.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_hid_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_hid_fast.h), [`input.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/input.c), [`frontend_android.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/frontend_android.c), [`motion_fusion.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/motion_fusion.c), [`hid_feedback.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/hid_feedback.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w23.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w23.h), [`test_android_ui_aspect.py`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/tests/test_android_ui_aspect.py), [`test_framegen_adaptive_publication.py`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/tests/test_framegen_adaptive_publication.py) (`TOTK_HID_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — C11 `_Atomic`-word seqlock slots `totk_pad_fast_slot` (bypassed on `edges != 0` and chord `CHORD_HELD_BACK`/`CHORD_TAP`), `totk_motion_fast_ring` (`32` slots, zero-syscall when `!live`, `<200ms` freshness check), `totk_kb_tracker` refcounted keycodes (`40`/`49`, `48`/`53`), `totk_vib_fast_state` consecutive-zero rumble elision preserving every non-zero send (`250 ms` `g_want_at` timeout) + mute reset, and fixed CI fixtures `test_android_ui_aspect.py` & `test_framegen_adaptive_publication.py`.

### Optimization 26 — AoI (`DM_SVC_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_SVC_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Thread / Handle / Event Signal-Clear Pools & Seqlock / Bounded `QueryMemory` + `GetInfo` SVC Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Global Lock Contention on Thread/Event/Memory SVCs and Byte-by-Byte Alias Scans):**
  - **AoI — `G` & `g_map_lock` (`dm/host/hle_thread.c`, `dm/host/hle_svc.c`):** Hot HLE SVCs (`GetThreadPriority`/`GetThreadCoreMask` `0x0C`/`0x0E`, `GetThreadId` `0x25`, `SignalEvent`/`ClearEvent`/`ResetSignal` `0x11`/`0x12`/`0x17`, `QueryMemory` `0x06`, `GetInfo` `0x29`, and `thr_limit_used`) serialized guest threads behind global mutexes `G` and `g_map_lock`, while blocking waits (`0x18`) acquired `G` every loop.
  - **AoC — `lock`, `memory_lock` & Byte-Wise Alias Scans (`games/totk/runtime/threads.c`, `games/totk/runtime/services.c`):** `GetThreadPriority`/`GetThreadCoreMask`/`GetThreadId` (`0x0C`/`0x0E`/`0x25`), `SignalEvent`/`ClearEvent`/`ResetSignal` (`0x11`/`0x12`/`0x17`), `runtime_handle_get` for `O_SESSION`/`O_TMEM`, `QueryMemory` (`0x06`), `GetInfo` (`0x29`), and contiguous-zero alias-page scans serialized under global `lock` and `memory_lock`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/svc_fast.h`](../dm/host/svc_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_thread.c`](../dm/host/hle_thread.c), [`dm/host/hle_svc.c`](../dm/host/hle_svc.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_svc_fast_w24.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_svc_fast_w24.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_SVC_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Fence-free C11 atomics (`svc_fast_thr_table` + `counted_live`/`thr_limit_used`, static 4096-slot ABA-safe `svc_fast_ev_sys` with `wsync_count` Dekker handshake and `*already_set` wake safety, odd/even `g_map_seq` + 16-slot TLS `svc_fast_qmem_tls` scoped to `[g_as, g_as+g_reserve)` or `state == 0xB`, and lock-free non-lifted `GetInfo` preserving live `big_bss_bytes()`).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_svc_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_svc_fast.h), [`threads.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/threads.c), [`services.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/services.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w24.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w24.h) (`TOTK_SVC_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`, `#ifndef GAME_BOTW` preserved in `reported_core`) — Fence-free C11 atomics (`totk_svc_thr_table`, `totk_svc_h_table` `O_SESSION`/`O_TMEM`, static 4096-slot `totk_svc_ev_sys` + `wsync_count` Dekker handshake, `_Atomic` `heap_size`/`alias_bytes` + odd/even `g_mem_seq` + TLS `totk_qmem_tls` when `g_smap_count == 0`, and `memcpy`-safe 8-byte word-skipping `totk_alias_scan_run()`).

### Optimization 27 — AoI (`DM_TIME_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_TIME_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Seqlock Time Context / Calendar / Timezone Caches, HIPC Session Bitset & Bounded IPC Serialization

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Per-Call Clock Syscalls, TZ/localtime_r Conversions, `/etc/localtime` `readlink`, Session Locks & Full-Buffer IPC Memset/Memcpy):**
  - **AoI — `hle_time.c` & `hle_sf.c` (`dm/host/hle_time.c`, `dm/host/hle_sf.c`):** `h_time_GetContext` serialized under static mutex `mu`; `h_time_ToCalendarTime` called `localtime_r` + `LOG` init on every conversion; `h_time_Snapshot` invoked `tzset()` plus redundant clock/calendar lookups; `h_sf_Send` acquired `g_sf_mu` solely to track `seen` per request.
