  - **AoC — `time_host.c` & `ipc.c` (`games/totk/runtime/time_host.c`, `games/totk/runtime/ipc.c`):** `steady_seconds` re-queried `mach_timebase_info` on macOS, `fill_calendar` called `localtime_r`/`gmtime_r` repeatedly, `location_name` ran `readlink("/etc/localtime", ...)` every call, `snapshot_create` formatted local calendar twice, and `ipc.c` zeroed/copied the entire `0x100`-byte `IpcRequest`/`IpcResponse` and IPC message buffer on small replies.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/time_fast.h`](../dm/host/time_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_time.c`](../dm/host/hle_time.c), [`dm/host/hle_sf.c`](../dm/host/hle_sf.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_time_fast_w25.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_time_fast_w25.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_TIME_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Fence-free C11 atomics (`time_fast_ctx_slot`, `(posix_sec, tz_tag)` `time_fast_cal_slot`, `time_fast_loc_slot`, deduplicated `get_context_at`/`to_calendar_tz` + single `TZ` tag in `h_time_Snapshot`, atomic `fixed_time()`/`user_clock()`, and 64-command bitset + fallback `time_fast_sf_seen` in `hle_sf.c`).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_time_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_time_fast.h), [`time_host.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/time_host.c), [`ipc.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/ipc.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w25.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w25.h) (`TOTK_TIME_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Fence-free C11 atomics (`totk_time_cal_cache` [UTC+local slots], `totk_time_loc_cache` skipping `/etc/localtime` `readlink`, packed atomic `mach_timebase_info`, single-calendar-fill `snapshot_create`, partial `totk_ipc_req_reset_fast`/`totk_ipc_resp_reset_fast`, and exact `totk_ipc_respond_bytes(woff, nwords, MSG_BYTES)` reply sizing).

### Optimization 28 — AoI (`DM_GATE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_GATE_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Gate Dispatch Table, 64-Way Seqlock Module/Objmap Caches & QueryMemory Consolidation

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Linear Module Range Scans, Binary Search Across Gate Tables, Mutex-Gated Object Lookups & Repeated Platform Checks):**
  - **AoI — `hle_os.c`, `loader.c` & `hle_svc.c` (`dm/host/hle_os.c`, `dm/host/loader.c`, `dm/host/hle_svc.c`):** `objmap_get` acquired `g_om_lock` on every object handle lookup; `now_ns()` re-checked a function-local timebase struct and scaled when `numer == denom`; `guest_ticks()` scanned all loaded modules via `dm_sdk_ounce()` every call; `module_of()` linearly scanned `g_mod[0..g_nmod)` for every PC/pointer query; and `QueryMemory` called `module_of(a)` twice per module hit.
  - **AoC — `loader.c` (`games/totk/runtime/loader.c`):** `runtime_dispatch` scanned `modules[0..NM)` and binary-searched per-module `gates[0..ngate)` on every guest->host gate transition, while `runtime_module_offset` linearly scanned `modules[0..NM)` for stack/diagnostic PC resolution.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/gate_fast.h`](../dm/host/gate_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_os.c`](../dm/host/hle_os.c), [`dm/host/loader.c`](../dm/host/loader.c), [`dm/host/hle_svc.c`](../dm/host/hle_svc.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_gate_fast_w26.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_gate_fast_w26.c), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_GATE_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Fence-free C11 atomics (`gate_fast_om_cache` 64-slot seqlock+generation cache on `objmap_*`, packed atomic `tb_pack` + `numer == denom` identity fast path in `now_ns`, `gate_fast_ounce_slot` keyed by `g_nmod`, `gate_fast_mod_idx` overall `[lo, hi)` fast-miss span + 64-slot 64 KiB-chunk seqlock memo in `module_of`, and single `module_of(a)` call in `QueryMemory`). Also fixed `dm_sk_poll` multi-slice eventfd wait deadlines in `dm/host/hle_net.c` + `dm/host/tests/t_net_eventfd.c`.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_gate_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_gate_fast.h), [`loader.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/loader.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w26.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w26.h) (`TOTK_GATE_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Flat open-addressed `totk_gate_table` published with release semantics before guest launch (O(1) `(ret_abs -> Gate*, mod_name)` lookup with binary-search fallback) and `totk_mod_cache` overall `[lo, hi)` fast-reject + 64-slot 64 KiB-chunk seqlock memo in `runtime_module_offset`.

### Optimization 29 — AoI (`DM_FS_LOOKUP_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_FS_LOOKUP_FAST=1`): Lock-Free RomFS/NUP Binary-Search Lookup Caches, Mount Name Table & Write-Once Directory Probe Memo

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Repeated RomFS/NUP Binary Searches, Linear Mount Scans, Path Normalization Allocations & Mutex-Gated File Handle Lookups):**
  - **AoI — `hle_fs.c` (`dm/host/hle_fs.c`):** Guest filesystem open/stat/case/transaction paths repeatedly ran `fs_lookup_split_path`-style normalization + linear scans over mounted `g_mnt[]` entries and repeated NUP archive binary searches for hot assets and negative `ENOENT` probes.
  - **AoC — `romfs.c` & `fs_host.c` (`games/totk/runtime/romfs.c`, `games/totk/runtime/fs_host.c`):** `romfs_open_path` and `romfs_get_entry_type` repeated `file_index()` binary searches across the RomFS tree and `folder_exists()` probes on every miss; `fs_host.c` linearly scanned `mounts[0..16)` with per-call `strlen()` and acquired `lock` in `file_lookup`/`save_touched`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/fs_lookup_fast.h`](../dm/host/fs_lookup_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_fs.c`](../dm/host/hle_fs.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_fs_lookup_fast_w27.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_fs_lookup_fast_w27.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_FS_LOOKUP_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Fence-free C11 atomics: `fs_lookup_hash64` (`8`-byte `memcpy` word hashing), `fs_lookup_split_path` (single-pass forward-slash & dot-norm guard), `fs_lookup_mnt_table` (`g_fs_mnt_fast` replacing linear mount scans in path resolution and case/transaction lookups), and write-once epoch-tagged `fs_lookup_path_cache` (`g_fs_path_fast` memoizing positive and `ENOENT` NUP index results).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_fs_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_fs_fast.h), [`romfs.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/romfs.c), [`fs_host.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/fs_host.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w27.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w27.h) (`TOTK_FS_LOOKUP_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Fence-free C11 atomics: `totk_fs_hash64` (`8`-byte `memcpy`), write-once `totk_fs_rom_cache` (`g_rom_fast` with monotonic `_Atomic uint8_t dstate` memoizing file/overlay/`ENOENT` and `folder_exists` directory probes across `romfs_open_path`/`romfs_get_entry_type`, bumped by `totk_fs_rom_bump` on overlay mount), `totk_fs_mnt_table` (`mnt_fast`), and packed atomic `fd_fast` (`totk_fs_fd_pack`/`totk_fs_fd_unpack_*`) bypassing mutex locks on hot `file_lookup`/`save_touched`.

### Optimization 30 — AoI (`DM_OE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_OE_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Applet Notification Poll, Pending State-Message Coalescing, Tear-Free Volume Balance & Language/Operation-Mode Seqlocks

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex-Locked Empty Notification Polls, Duplicate Pending Applet State Broadcasts, Locked Audio Balance Loads & Per-Call Environment Lookups):**
  - **AoI — `hle_oe.c` (`dm/host/hle_oe.c`):** Frame-paced `h_oe_TryPopMsg` polling acquired `g_mq_lock` and called `mq_ev_sync` even when `g_mqn == 0` and `g_mq_ev_on == 0`; `oe_script_ensure` hit `pthread_once` on every poll; `opmode`/`gfx_scale`/`gfx_size` used unlocked lazy initialization; and `dm_lang_code`/`region` re-read `DM_LANG` via `getenv` with string comparisons on repeated queries.
  - **AoC — `applet_host.c` & `services.c` (`games/totk/runtime/applet_host.c`, `games/totk/runtime/services.c`):** `TryPopNotificationMessage` unconditionally locked `lock` on empty frames; duplicate pending state-invalidation messages (`15`, `30`, `31`) accumulated in the queue; `GetExpectedVolumeBalance`/`SetExpectedVolumeBalance` took `lock`; `report_save` updated `reports` under race; and `handheld_mode()` / `desired_language()` repeatedly scanned environment variables via `getenv` and `strcmp`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/oe_fast.h`](../dm/host/oe_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_oe.c`](../dm/host/hle_oe.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_oe_fast_w28.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_oe_fast_w28.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_OE_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Fence-free C11 atomics: `oe_fast_try_pop_empty` (lock-free `g_mqn == 0 && g_mq_ev_on == 0` bypass preserving `t_oe.c` `ev_sig` clearing when `g_mq_ev_on == 1` and `g_mqn = 0` resets), `oe_script_ensure` acquire guard ahead of `pthread_once`, `oe_fast_load_int`/`oe_fast_load_u32` (`memory_order_acquire`/`release`) on `g_opmode`/`g_gfx_scale`/`g_gfx_size`, `oe_fast_def_res`, and `oe_fast_lang_slot` (`g_lang_fast` seqlock caching `DM_LANG` -> `(cu, r)` while preserving first-call log output and `setenv` invalidation).
  - **AoC:** [`totk_oe_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_oe_fast.h), [`applet_host.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/applet_host.c), [`services.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/services.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w28.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w28.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_OE_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Fence-free C11 atomics: `totk_oe_mq_try_empty` bypassing `lock` on empty notification polls, `totk_oe_mq_push_locked` coalescing duplicate pending state-invalidation messages (`15`, `30`, `31`) while preserving per-occurrence `95`, XOR-encoded zero-init tear-free `_Atomic uint64_t` `totk_oe_balance`, `_Atomic uint64_t reports` in `report_save`, CAS `_Atomic int` in `services.c:handheld_mode()` + `totk_oe_opmode_to_console`, and 2-word `(tag_lo, tag_hi)` byte-loop digest seqlock `totk_oe_lang_slot` in `services.c:desired_language()`.

### Optimization 31 — AoI (`DM_AUDREN_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_AUDREN_FAST=1`): Single-Pass Command-List Sink Scan, Division-Free 3:2 Upsampler, Waiter-Gated Condition Broadcasts, Sparse `AudioRenderer` Output & Hoisted Reverb/Delay DSP

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (`AudioRenderSystem` / `AudioRenderer` Per-Frame Scans, Unconditional Mutex/Zeroing Traffic & Inner-Loop Redundant Work):**
  - **AoI — `hle_audren.c` (`dm/host/hle_audren.c`):** `find_sink` scanned every `ncmd` command twice (first walking for histograms, then walking again to find the enabled `DeviceSink`); `up_3_2` recomputed `(t + 2u) / 3u` division/modulo inside the inner sample loop for 32 kHz -> 48 kHz frames; `cq_popped` locked `g_cq_mu` and called `pthread_cond_broadcast(&g_cq_cv)` on every 5 ms frame even when zero guest waiters were sleeping; `render_lock_ensure` hit `pthread_once` on every acquire; and `rev_find` locked `g_rev_mu` on every lookup over `4` renderer slots.
  - **AoC — `audio_renderer.c`, `audio_reverb.c`, `audio_effects.c` (`games/totk/runtime/audio_renderer.c`, `games/totk/runtime/audio_reverb.c`, `games/totk/runtime/audio_effects.c`):** `request_update` zeroed the full `12+ KiB` output buffer (including header, pool, and voice tables immediately overwritten), unconditionally rewrote every `AudrenPoolIn` slot, and ran full `308`-byte `AudrenVoiceInV2` unpack/validate/copy on idle `SV_STOPPED` voices (`~104` of `128`); `audio_reverb_process` re-dispatched channel routing and modulo delay wrapping across `20` early taps per sample; and `delay_process` ran unhoisted general `n`-channel delay loops and `float` parameter divisions per sample for the dominant stereo (`n == 2`) case.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/audren_fast.h`](../dm/host/audren_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_audren.c`](../dm/host/hle_audren.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_audren_fast_w29.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_audren_fast_w29.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_AUDREN_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Single-pass layout-specialized `audren_fast_find_sink` (`audren_scan_sdk16`/`sdk6`/`sdk21`) validating all `ncmd` bounds and locating the first enabled `DeviceSink` in one walk; division-free unrolled `audren_fast_up_3_2`; `seq_cst` waiter-gated `cq_popped` (`audren_cq_should_wake`); acquire-guarded `render_lock_ensure`; and lock-free `audren_rev_find` across the `4`-slot `audren_rev_slot` table.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_audren_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_audren_fast.h), [`audio_renderer.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/audio_renderer.c), [`audio_reverb.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/audio_reverb.c), [`audio_effects.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/audio_effects.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w29.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w29.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_AUDREN_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Sparse `totk_audren_zero_tail`, dirty-checked batch `totk_audren_pools_update`, `totk_audren_voice_is_noop_idle` + `totk_audren_voice_v2_unpack` in `request_update_voices` (preserving `SV_STOPPED == 0` and uninitialized `adpcm.buffer == -1` checks), pre-selected `totk_reverb3d_route` + `totk_reverb3d_early_accum`, hoisted `reverb_process` gains, and specialized `totk_delay_stereo_run`.

### Optimization 32 — AoI (`DM_VK_WORKER_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_UPLOAD_FAST=1`): Inline Replay-Worker Job Buffers, Single-Thread `qs_sub` Slab Pool, Iterative 64-Bit Sync-Mask Scanner, Waiter-Gated Condition Wakes, Lock-Free Upload-Pool Helpers & Single-Geom Stack Deswizzle Planner

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Worker/Upload Queue Allocation Churn, Unconditional Condition-Variable Wakes & Repeated Geometry Work):**
  - **AoI — `vka_worker.c` & `vka_qsched.h` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_worker.c`, `dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched.h`):** Every `mtl_sub_commit` / `worker_main` submission performed 3 heap allocations (`j.h`, `j.ws` realloc growth, and `calloc(1, sizeof(qs_sub))`) freed later inside `qs_run_one`; `fence_syncs` decoded every draw/bind/push record via recursive branches instead of skipping non-sync opcodes with a 64-bit bitmask (`OP_COUNT_ <= 64`) and an explicit stack; and `push`, `worker_main`, and `mtl_event_cpu_signal` called `pthread_cond_broadcast(&g_cv)` unconditionally even when neither worker nor producer was sleeping.
  - **AoC — `vk_upload_pool.c` & `vk_res.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/vk_upload_pool.c`, `games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c`):** `upload_pool_helpers()` locked/unlocked `g_mu` on every texture upload; `upload_pool_deswizzle` ran `plan()` twice (recomputing `nvn_deswizzle_geom` and 64-bit divisions per subresource before and after `g_runs` reallocation); `vk_res.c` heap-allocated `VkBufferImageCopy` and `upload_sub` arrays via `calloc`/`malloc` even for `<= 16`-subresource textures; and workers called `pthread_cond_signal(&g_done)` unconditionally even when the host drain loop had already finished all runs without waiting.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_worker_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_worker_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_worker.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_worker.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_worker_fast_w30.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_worker_fast_w30.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_WORKER_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Inline `vka_job_buf` storage (`<= 16` handles / `<= 8` `WAIT_SYNC` snapshots, pointer-free value copy), single-thread `vka_sub_pool` (`cap=64`) released via `qs.free_sub = vka_sub_release`, iterative 64-bit `VKA_SYNC_SCAN_MASK` sync scanner (`ret[16]`, zero recursion), and mutex-tracked `vka_worker_wait_state` condvar wake elision (`worker_idle_wait`, `worker_parked_wait`, `prod_waiters`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_upload_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_upload_fast.h), [`vk_upload_pool.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_upload_pool.c), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w30.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w30.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_UPLOAD_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — Acquire/release `g_limit_fast` in `upload_pool_helpers()`, single-geom-pass `totk_upload_geom_pass` + `totk_upload_emit_runs` using shifts (`>> 6`, `>> (th + 3)`), fast path `block_row >= UPLOAD_POOL_RUN_BYTES`, stack `geoms[96]` / `stack_runs[128]`, single-helper `pthread_cond_signal(&g_work)` + `g_done_waiting`-gated `pthread_cond_signal(&g_done)`, and inline `stack_reg[16]` / `stack_subs[16]` in `vk_res.c:upload_staged`.

### Optimization 33 — AoI (`DM_MOVIE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_MOVIE_FAST=1`): SIMD/SWAR NV12 & RGBA->BGRA Video Pixels, Lock-Free `active_mask` Seqlock Lookup, O(1) Slot Bitmasks, Idle `movie_host_tick` Short-Circuit & Waiter-Gated Pump Wakes

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Scalar Video Pixel Packing, Mutex-Linear Decoder Scans & Redundant Pump Signaling):**
  - **AoI — `hle_movie.c` & `hle_movie_nv12.h` (`dm/host/hle_movie.c`, `dm/host/hle_movie_nv12.h`):** Cropped NV12/RGBA uploads cleared the entire frame buffer before copying valid rows/columns; RGBA->BGRA byte swapping ran scalar loops; x86_64 NV12 UV interleaving lacked `__SSE2__` `_mm_unpacklo_epi8`/`_mm_unpackhi_epi8` + SWAR paths and `arm64` used `vld4q_u8` instead of `vqtbl1q_u8`; `vp9_size` scanned bits one-by-one instead of 128-bit big-endian bitfield reads; free/rendered slot scans walked all `MD_NOUT` entries linearly; and `md_pump_async` signaled `d->pkc` even when a wake was pending or the pump thread was busy.
  - **AoC — `movie_host.c` & `movie_nv12.h` (`games/totk/runtime/movie_host.c`, `games/totk/runtime/movie_nv12.h`):** `movie_host_tick()` locked `g_lock` on every frame even with zero live decoders; `find()` acquired `g_lock` and scanned all `MAX_MOVIES` (`16`) slots twice per `h_get_output_md` call; `free_out()`, `decode_one()`, and `h_release_output()` looped over `OUT_BUFS` (`8`) instead of maintaining `held_mask`/`queued_mask` bitmasks; and `totk_nv12_interleave_row` lacked `__SSE2__` + SWAR interleaving on non-NEON hosts.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/host/movie_fast.h`](../dm/host/movie_fast.h), [`dm/host/hle_movie_nv12.h`](../dm/host/hle_movie_nv12.h), [`dm/host/hle_movie.c`](../dm/host/hle_movie.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_movie_fast_w31.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_movie_fast_w31.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_MOVIE_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — Border-only `dm_movie_rgba_pad_borders`, `dm_nv12_uv_simd_span` + `dm_nv12_uv_swar_span` (`__aarch64__` NEON / `__SSE2__` unpack + 8/4-byte SWAR), `dm_movie_rgba_to_bgra_simd` (`arm64` `vqtbl1q_u8` / `__SSE2__`), `dm_movie_vp9_size_fast`, `used_mask`/`rnd_cli_mask` with `dm_movie_first_free_slot`, and `dm_movie_pump_wake` waiter-gated elision.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_movie_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_movie_fast.h), [`movie_nv12.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/movie_nv12.h), [`movie_host.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/movie_host.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w31.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w31.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_MOVIE_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `totk_movie_table` seqlock with `_Atomic uint32_t active_mask`, `totk_movie_has_live` idle `movie_host_tick` skip (`#ifdef TOTK_MOVIE_HOST_TU`), single-`find` `get_output_fmt_for_movie`/`get_output_for_movie` split, O(1) `held_mask`/`queued_mask`, and `totk_nv12_interleave_simd` + SWAR (`__aarch64__` NEON / `__SSE2__` + little-endian 8/4-byte zip).

### Optimization 34 — AoI (`DM_VK_IMGMEM_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_IMGMEM_FAST=1`): SIMD/SWAR Free-Extent Search with Cached `max_n`, Inline Open-Addressed `vkz_tab` Hash Lookup, Bit-Transposed Memory-Type LUT, O(1) `type_mask[32]` Block Jump & Binary-Search Coalescing Image Allocator

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Linear Vulkan Image-Memory Free-List Scans, `vkz_tab` Hash Heap Churn & Repeated `vkGetPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties` Bit Loops):**
  - **AoI — `vka_extents.h`, `vka_zshare.h`, `vka_imgmem.c` & `vka_dev.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `vke_alloc` linearly scanned every free extent without tracking `max_n` or skipping `4`-wide non-fitting spans; `vkz_tab` ran `calloc`/`free` on separate key/value tables and linearly probed with cold bucket walks; `vka_pick_memtype` looped over all Vulkan memory types and property bits on every allocation instead of consulting a precomputed 32-entry property mask LUT; and empty slab retirement in `vka_imgmem.c` lacked a fast threshold check.
  - **AoC — `vk_res.c` & `nvn_vk.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `VkImgMemBlock` / block pool selection linearly walked every pool block and non-matching memory type instead of maintaining a `type_mask[32]` bitmask per memory type and cached `max_free` per block; free spans were searched sequentially instead of skipping 4 spans at a time and carving in place; span frees linearly scanned and coalesced adjacent regions instead of binary-searching by offset; and `vk_find_memtype` looped linearly over `VkPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties` bits on every allocation.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_imgmem_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_imgmem_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_extents.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_extents.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_zshare.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_zshare.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_imgmem.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_imgmem.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_imgmem_fast_w32.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_imgmem_fast_w32.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_IMGMEM_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vke_scan_fit4` (`__aarch64__` NEON / `__SSE2__` / SWAR) + cached `max_n` (`vke_invalidate_max`), zero-alloc inline open-addressed `vkz_slot` hash table helpers for `vkz_tab`, bit-transposed `vka_memtype_lut`, and `vka_imem_empty_should_free`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_imgmem_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_imgmem_fast.h), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w32.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w32.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_IMGMEM_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `totk_mem_span`/`totk_mem_block`, O(1) `type_mask[32]` block-bit jump (`ctz64`) + cached `max_free` block skip, pow2 bitmask `totk_mem_align_up`, `4x` span skip + in-place carve (`totk_imgmem_block_alloc`), O(log N) binary-search coalescing `totk_imgmem_block_free`, and `totk_memtype_lut`.
### Optimization 35 — AoI (`DM_VK_SHLIB_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_SEED_FAST=1`): Branchless Prefetch `dmsh_vk.lib` Binary Search, Single-Load Big-Endian Key, Zero-Alloc SPIR-V `fout_comps` / `pool_fb` Prescan, Bitset-LUT `TOTKVKP1` Monotonic Pack Validator, Slab-Allocated Inline Seed Inventory & O(1) Intrusive Warm Queue

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Linear Byte-Loop Shader Keys, Per-Lookup Heap Churn & O(N log N) Startup Pack Validation):**
  - **AoI — `vka_shlib.c` & `vka_spvpatch.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `vka_shlib_key` folded 8 metadata bytes in a scalar loop and `vka_shlib_find` ran a branchy midpoint loop without prefetching; `vka_spv_fout_comps` allocated `calloc(bound, ...)` twice on every fragment module to map `Component` decorations while `vka_spv_pool_fb` walked every instruction even when no `UniformConstant` / binding matched, and `put_n` grew `vec_t` one word at a time.
  - **AoC — `vk_seed_pack.h` & `vk_seed_io.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `vk_seed_pack_valid_name` range-checked characters one byte at a time and `vk_seed_pack_validate` allocated `vk_seed_pack_range` and ran `qsort` even when `TOTKVKP1` offsets were already monotonic; `vk_seed_io.c` allocated individual `seed_file` nodes into `nvn_hmap` (`klen = 84`) and unlinked the warm queue with an `O(W)` singly-linked scan.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shlib_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shlib_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shlib.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_shlib.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_shlib_fast_w33.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_shlib_fast_w33.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv), [`dm/tools/lint_c_baseline.tsv`](../dm/tools/lint_c_baseline.tsv) (`DM_VK_SHLIB_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_shlib_key_fast` (`__builtin_bswap64` / `memcpy`), branchless prefetching `vka_shlib_find_fast`, `vka_spv_vec_reserve` / `vka_spv_put_words`, zero-alloc two-pass `vka_spv_fout_comps_fast` (`vka_fout_pass1` + `vka_fout_pass2`), and read-only prescan `vka_spv_pool_fb_prescan`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_seed_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_seed_fast.h), [`vk_seed_pack.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_seed_pack.h), [`vk_seed_io.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_seed_io.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w33.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w33.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_SEED_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `totk_seed_valid_name_fast` / `totk_seed_valid_cstr_fast` (`256`-bit `k_seed_char_lut[4]`), `totk_seed_pack_scan_fast` monotonic fast path (`totk_seed_check_overlaps` fallback only if out-of-order), open-addressed `totk_seed_idx` (`totk_seed_hash`, `totk_seed_idx_get`, `totk_seed_idx_put_unique`), `seed_slab` packing, intrusive `totk_seed_warm_link` O(1) warm queue, and atomic `totk_seed_ready_decode`.
### Optimization 36 — AoI (`DM_VK_PCACHE_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_PCACHE_FAST=1`): Zero-`sscanf`/`snprintf` Pipeline-Cache Stamp I/O, Partial-Key Prewarm Scans, Lock-Free Seqlock Layout Front-Cache & Active-Prefix Key Hashing, Bitset-LUT PSO Keys, Open-Addressed Collector Index, O(1) Demand Unlink & Word-Bounded Dirty Bitmaps

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (`sscanf`/`snprintf` Stamp Loops, Unbounded Key Hashes, Mutex-Guarded Layout Cache & O(W) PSO Demand Scans):**
  - **AoI — `vka_pckeep.h` & `vka_pso.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `vka_pck_parse` copied the stamp buffer and called `sscanf` per shader line plus `qsort` even when keys were monotonic, while `vka_pck_format` always sorted both `keep` and `run` arrays and called `snprintf` per line; `vka_pso.c` scanned full `vka_gkey` structs (`vk_pw_next`), hashed/compared full padded `lkey` buffers including unused bindings (`layout_get`), and acquired `g_mu` on every `vka_layout_of` lookup.
  - **AoC — `vk_pso_keys.h`, `vk_pso_runtime.inc` & `vk_draw.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `pso_name` called `strnlen` and repeated `strchr` byte scans for hex PSO keys while `pso_valid` repeated byte-by-byte bounds checks; `g_pso_keys` used generic `nvn_hmap` (`klen = 784`), demand promotion in `vk_pso_runtime.inc` walked the prewarm `g_pw_head` singly-linked list `O(W)`, and `g_pw_done` dirty/completed accounting scanned full byte arrays bit-by-bit.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pcache_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pcache_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pckeep.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pckeep.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_pcache_fast_w34.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_pcache_fast_w34.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_PCACHE_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_pck_parse_fast` (single-pass hex/decimal scanner with `vka_pck_is_strict_asc` monotonicity fast path), `vka_pck_format_fast` (`vka_put_hex16` / `vka_put_dec` + merge without re-sorting already-ordered `keep`), `vka_pw_next_fast` (bounded `offsetof(vka_gkey, va)` copy for non-`vin_static` records), lock-free 256-slot seqlock `vka_lshort_cache` (`vka_lshort_get` / `vka_lshort_put`) for `vka_layout_of`, and active-prefix `km_get_prefix` / `vka_lkey_eq_fast` (`offsetof(lkey, b) + k.n * sizeof k.b[0]`).

### Optimization 37 — AoI (`DM_VK_EXEC_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_REC_FAST=1`): 64-Slot Front-Cached Render-Condition Hash Table, 64-Bit Opcode Dispatch Bitmasks, Generation-Cached Env Gate, O(1) CLZ Recorder Slab Classes, 8-Slot Descriptor-Stock Memo, 160-Byte Dynamic State Short-Circuit & Bitmask Vertex Loop

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Linear Address/Descriptor Lookups, Branchy Per-Opcode/Per-Vertex Loops & Repeated Cold Checks):**
  - **AoI — `vka_exec.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `rcm_find` and `rcm_note` linearly scanned `g_rcm` on every render-condition lookup, `rcm_find` re-checked `DM_VK_RC_QUERY_MEM` via `getenv` (`VKA_ENV`) on hits, and `exec` / `ind_scan_collect_both` / `memu_allowed` ran chained opcode branches and indirect/memory-tracking pre-checks across every guest op.
  - **AoC — `vk_record.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `storage_class` iterated buckets linearly and `storage_get` left active slabs unpromoted; `vk_desc_layout_fits` scanned `vk_desc_pool_sizes` per binding while `vk_desc_stock_take` went straight to `nvn_hmap` on every descriptor set; `record_dynamic_emit_inner` always compared/updated state without a word short-circuit; `record_job` scanned all `17` vertex bits serially and `job_allocate` zeroed `guard_cache` even when `had_guards == 0`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_exec.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_exec_fast_w35.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_exec_fast_w35.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`dm/tools/tests/test_vka_spec_freeze.py`](../dm/tools/tests/test_vka_spec_freeze.py), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_EXEC_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_rcm_front64` (`g_rcm_fc`: 64-slot direct-mapped front cache) + `vka_rcm_hash64` splitmix64 open-addressed `vka_rcm_find_fast` / `vka_rcm_note_fast`, `vka_rc_qmem_sync_on` keyed on `vka_env_gen`, 64-bit opcode bitmasks (`vka_op_bit_test`, `vka_op_memu_allowed_fast`, `vka_op_need_settle_fast`, `vka_op_rp_nop_fast`, `vka_op_ind_write_fast`), `vka_exec_idx_skip_pre`, and helper extraction (`exec_pre_pass`, `exec_report_counter`, `exec_draw_idx_pre`; `exec` under 60 lines).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_rec_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_rec_fast.h), [`vk_record.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_record.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w35.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w35.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_REC_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `totk_rec_storage_class_fast` (`__builtin_clzll`) + MRU slab head-promotion in `storage_get`, `totk_desc_pool_type_slot` + 8-slot direct-mapped `totk_desc_stock_memo8` (`totk_desc_stock_memo_get` / `totk_desc_stock_memo_put` backing `vk_desc_stock_take`), 160-byte `totk_rec_dyn_unchanged` word short-circuit (`_Static_assert(sizeof(vk_record_dynamic) == 160u)`), `totk_rec_vmask` `__builtin_ctz` vertex loop (`record_draw_vertices` / `record_one_vertex`), and `totk_rec_need_guard_zero` in `job_allocate`.
### Optimization 38 — AoI (`DM_VK_PRESENT_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_POST_FAST=1`): Generation-Cached Present Config, Descriptor-Set Memo & Revision Masking, Seqlock Presentation/Ready History Rings & Waiter-Tracked Condvar Wake Elision

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Per-Frame Present Re-Validation, Redundant Descriptor Writes & Unfiltered Condvar Wakes):**
  - **AoI — `vka_present.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `present_one` / `frame_rate` / `present_fmt` / `fsr_copy` polled up to `13` env knobs (`getenv`/`VKA_ENV`/`sscanf` on `DM_VK_PICTURE_ASPECT`) per present frame, re-wrote unchanged `g_rot` / `g_fsr` descriptor sets every frame, synced `g_pres` / `g_pq_*` progress behind `g_pmu`, and broadcast `g_pcv` with `0` waiters.
  - **AoC — `vk_post.c` + `nvn_vk.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `vk_post_run` allocated fresh transient descriptor sets via `vk_alloc_set` and wrote all `4` combined image sampler bindings on every post stage (`POST_EDGE`..`POST_SGSR`) regardless of unchanged LUT (`P.area`/`P.search`) or slot intermediate revisions, `vk_present_ready_ns` acquired `g_mu` on every poll, and `nvn_vk.c` broadcast `g_cv` on every submission/completion even when `0` waiters slept on `g_cv`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_present_fast_w36.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_present_fast_w36.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_PRESENT_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_pres_cfg_sync(vka_env_gen)` + bounded `vka_parse_aspect_fast`, `vka_pres_dset_memo` (`vka_pres_rot_need_write` / `vka_pres_fsr_src_need_write` / `vka_pres_fsr_mid_need_write`) skipping unchanged `g_rot`/`g_fsr` descriptor writes (`swap_destroy(1)` resets), seqlock `vka_pres_ring512` (`g_pres_fast`), lockless `g_pq_sync` (`in_sh`/`done_sh` fast wait-skip & progress), waiter-gated `pcv_wake()`, and `present_one` split into `<=60`-line helpers (`present_frame_prep`, `present_fit_box`, `present_record_img`, `present_drain_gate`, `present_submit_swap`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_post_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_post_fast.h), [`vk_post.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_post.c), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w36.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w36.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_POST_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `post_pool_init()` persistent `slot_set[POST_SLOTS][POST_STAGES]` (gated on `P.aa || P.scale`) + `post_image::rev` & `totk_post_dset_mask` (`POST_EDGE` writes `0..1`, `WEIGHTS`/`BLEND` write `0` on matching `aux_rev`/`lut_rev`, `RCAS` writes `0` on matching `src_rev`, caller `EASU`/`SGSR` writes `4` so destroyed views never dangle), lockless `totk_present_ready_ring` (`g_present_ready_fast`) backing `vk_present_ready_ns`, waiter-gated `nvn_cv_wake()` across all `12` `g_cv` broadcasts, and `vk_post_init` / `completion_thread` / `slot_submit_inner` extracted `<=60` lines.
### Optimization 39 — AoI (`DM_VK_PERF_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_PERF_FAST=1`): FD-Based Procfs/Sysfs Readers, Open-Addressed TID Delta Map, Quickselect Percentiles, Env-Generation Perf Snapshot, Occupancy/Critical Thread Bitmasks & Zero-Work Timeline Skip

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Buffered File/Scanf Telemetry Overhead, Quadratic TID Scans & Idle Frame Timeline Work):**
  - **AoI — `vka_perf.c` / `vka_pace.h` / `vka_gbar.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Periodic ADPF/pace sampling opened procfs/sysfs counters via `fopen`+`fscanf`/`sscanf` (`schedstat`, `status`, `/proc/self/task/<tid>/stat`, `scaling_{cur,max}_freq`, KGSL/devfreq GPU clock files), ran `O(N^2)` linear searches across `ts` snapshot entries in `ts_busiest`, ran quadratic insertion sort (`vkpace_pct`) up to `4` times per pace log tick, and polled `DM_VK_ADPF_GPU`/`DM_VK_ADPF_SPAN`/`DM_VK_PACE_LOG`/`DM_VK_BAR_NARROW` via `VKA_ENV` on every frame.
  - **AoC — `android_perf.c` + `frame_timeline.c` (`games/totk/runtime/`):** `totk_perf_thread` and `totk_perf_frame` linearly scanned all `256` `registry[]` slots plus repeated `O(256)` argmax passes to pick up to `6` non-critical busy threads, `frame_timeline.c` spun in a tight empty loop on `e->lock` and `memset`-cleared `totk_ft_active_work` / looked up `entry(frame)` even when `totk_ft_on()` was `0` or no work counters accrued, and `totk_perf_pacing`/`totk_perf_wait_end` entered span/lock checks when `end <= begin`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_perf_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_perf_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pace.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pace.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gbar.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gbar.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_perf.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_perf.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_perf_fast_w37.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_perf_fast_w37.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_PERF_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_perf_cfg_sync(&g_pcfg, vka_env_gen)` + `vka_env_cached_i32(&g_bar_narrow_cfg)` caching `DM_VK_ADPF_GPU`/`DM_VK_ADPF_SPAN`/`DM_VK_PACE_LOG`/`DM_VK_BAR_NARROW`, raw `open`/`read`/`close` (`vka_read_small_file`) + bounded ASCII parsers (`vka_parse_u64_one`, `vka_parse_stat_ut_st`), `512`-slot open-addressed `vka_tid_map` in `ts_busiest`, dual-rank `vka_pace_p50_p90` Quickselect (`vkpace_p50_p90`/`vka_pace_pct_fast`), and atomic `g_dirty` publish/consume in `vka_perf.c`.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_perf_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/totk_perf_fast.h), [`frame_timeline.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/frame_timeline.c), [`android_perf.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/runtime/android_perf.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w37.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w37.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_PERF_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — 256-bit `totk_reg_bmap` (`occ[4]`, `crit[4]`) with `totk_bmap_first_free` + `totk_bmap_pick_top6` CTZ top-6 selector (`perf_select_tids_fast`, `perf_select_tids_slow`, `perf_refresh_session` extracted `<=60` lines), Arch-specific `totk_spin_pause()` backoff in `lock_entry`, `totk_ft_work_begin`/`end` gating on active recording & `totk_ft_work_nonzero()`, and early-out on `end <= begin` in `totk_perf_pacing`/`totk_perf_wait_end`.
### Optimization 40 — AoI (`DM_VK_FG_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_FG_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Physical-Device Format Feature Cache, Generation-Tracked Framegen-Off Short-Circuit, Precompiled FSR3 Pass Metadata / Geometry & RCAS F16 Memos, LSFG Batched Descriptor Writes, O(1) Terminal Copy Hash Table & Persistent Diff-Masked Render Descriptors

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Repeated Framegen/Upscale Format Probes, Per-Frame Descriptor Churn & Uncached Pass/Copy Lookups):**
  - **AoI — `vka_framegen.c` / `vka_fsr3.c` / `lsfg_chain.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Every framegen-off tick repeated `vka_fps_fg` + state notifications and re-evaluated settings even when `g_fg_set_gen` was unchanged, `fg_fmt_features`/`f3_fmt_features` queried `vkGetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties` repeatedly for identical formats, `vka_fsr3.c` re-parsed `g_kinds[p]` character-by-character plus recomputed projection trig, log2 mip counts, `exp2f` RCAS constants, and FP16 conversions every dispatch alongside per-frame `VKA_ENV`/`VKA_ENVF` lookups, and `lsfg_chain_new` flushed `vkUpdateDescriptorSets` individually after every pass.
  - **AoC — `vk_framegen.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** Framegen queried `vkGetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties` repeatedly without caching, `fg_terminal_copy` linearly scanned `o->copy[0..o->copies-1]` up to `VK_FG_TERMINAL_DESCRIPTORS` times per binding, `vk_fg_terminal_owned_binding` issued one `vkUpdateDescriptorSets` call per write instead of batching until `vk_fg_terminal_owned_end`, `fg_render_record_slot` re-allocated transient `VkDescriptorSet`s and rewrote all `11` bindings on every frame, and `vk_fg_late` took `fg_stats_mu` around `late_skips++`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fg_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fg_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_framegen.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_framegen.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fsr3.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fsr3.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/lsfg/lsfg_chain.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/lsfg/lsfg_chain.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_fg_fast_w38.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_fg_fast_w38.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_FG_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_fmt_feat_cache` (`64`-slot lock-free CAS format-feature cache shared by `vka_framegen.c` and `vka_fsr3.c`), `vka_fg_off_tracker` generation-tracked idle short-circuit + `vka_fg_env_cache` (`vka_env_gen` snapshot of `DM_LSFG_MIN_FPS`/`DM_VK_FG_REFRESH_CAP`/`DM_LSFG_DUMP`/`DM_VK_FG_OWNFMT`), `vka_fsr3_compile_kinds` (`vka_fsr3_pass_meta`), `vka_fsr3_geom_sync` + `vka_fsr3_rcas_sync` + `vka_f32_to_f16_bits` in `vka_fsr3_fast_memo`, and `vka_lsfg_batch_ctl` (`VKA_LSFG_BATCH_MAX=256`) batching `lsfg_chain_new` descriptor updates.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_fg_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_fg_fast.h), [`vk_framegen.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_framegen.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w38.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w38.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_FG_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `totk_fg_fmt_cache` (`64`-slot lock-free format-feature cache), `totk_fg_term_ht` (`512`-slot stamped `O(1)` hash index for `fg_terminal_copy`), batched `fg_terminal_owned_flush_or_queue` flushed once in `vk_fg_terminal_owned_end`, `F.render_pool` + `totk_fg_render_slot` (`totk_fg_desc_diff_mask` updating only changed bindings with `descriptorCount=1`), atomic `F.late_skips` in `vk_fg_late`, and helper extractions (`vk_fg_stats`, `fg_handle_off`, `fsr3_fill_consts`, `fg_render_bind_prepare`) keeping all modified C functions `<=60` lines.
### Optimization 41 — AoI (`DM_VK_SPVPATCH_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_SPV_FAST=1`): Stack-Backed SPIR-V ID Tables, Pre-Function Declaration Bounds, Bulk Word-Run Emission, Single-Pass FragDepth/FragCoord/SSBO Short-Circuits & Non-Mutating `.vk` Reflection Parsing

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Pipeline/Shader-Load SPIR-V Heap Churn, Full-Module Rescans & Tokenized Reflection Parsing):**
  - **AoI — `vka_spvpatch.c` / `vka_memu_spv.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Shader rewriters (`vka_spv_vin_retype`, `vka_spv_fout_retype`, `vka_spv_subgroup_single`, `smod_open`/`smod_io`, `vka_memu_fs_input_locs`) issued repeated per-ID `calloc`/`malloc` allocations even when `bound <= 1024`, scanned full function bodies for declarations and `SpecId`s after `OpFunction`, and appended output SPIR-V words one `uint32_t` at a time via `put()`/`sb_put()`.
  - **AoC — `vk_shader.c` / `vk_varyings.h` / `vk_load_proof.h` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** Each shader load allocated per-ID heap scratch arrays in `vk_spirv_io`, `vk_load_certify`, `spirv_mark_stores`, `spirv_fout_locations`, and `spirv_fragcoord_xy` alongside heap `VkDescriptorSetLayoutBinding`, scanned past `OpFunction` in `patch_set`/`spirv_fout_locations`/`spirv_depth_nudge`/`spirv_fragcoord_xy` even when absent, and mutated `.vk` reflection text with `strtok_r` + `sscanf` per binding line inside monolithic `shader_load`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_spvpatch.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_spv.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_memu_spv.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_spvpatch_fast_w39.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_spvpatch_fast_w39.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_SPVPATCH_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_retype_tbl`, `vka_sg_tbl`, `vka_fs_locs_tbl`, and `smod_open`/`smod_io` stack-backed tables (`VKA_SPV_STACK_BOUND=1024`, `VKA_SPV_SMOD_STACK=1024` with heap fallback), pre-`OpFunction` bounds (`vka_vin_scan_fast`, `vka_fout_scan_fast`, `vka_sg_scan`, `vka_memu_fs_input_locs_fast`), bulk word-run `memcpy` flushes (`vka_spv_flush_run`, `vka_spv_put_words_w39`, `vka_sb_raw_fast`), and fast `vka_spv_spec_freeze_fast`.

### Optimization 42 — AoI (`DM_VK_OVL_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_OVL_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Overlay Generation Skip, Dirty-Row Staging Copy, GPU Frame-Window Memoization, Hash-Indexed GPU Profiler Aggregation, Branchless Cell Rasterization & FD-Backed `/proc`-`/sys` Telemetry

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Present-Thread Overlay Redraw/Upload Churn, O(N) GPU Profiler Scans & Telemetry File Re-Parsing):**
  - **AoI — `vka_present.c` / `vka_fps.c` / `vka_gprof.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Every swapchain present took `g_omu` in `ovl_draw` even when `nxhost_overlay_set` had not published a new generation, copied the entire RGBA staging surface unconditionally via `memcpy`, scanned `[f-41..f-9]` (`33` `mtl_gpu_ms` calls) in `vka_fps.c` `gpu_ms()`, and used linear array scans in `split_report` (`vka_gprof.c`) across shapes and 3-part `(tag, pkey, work)` pass keys.
  - **AoC — `vk_overlay.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `vk_overlay_raster` cleared `pixels` with a volatile/scalar loop, branched bit-by-bit inside glyph row rasterization in `raster_cell_mode`, and telemetry polling in `read_number` / `memory_mib` opened `FILE*` streams with `fscanf` / line-by-line `fgets` + `sscanf` across `/sys` and `/proc/{meminfo,self/status}`.

  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ovl_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ovl_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_present.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fps.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fps.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gprof.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_gprof.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_ovl_fast_w40.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_ovl_fast_w40.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_OVL_FAST=1`, `0` disables; live `VKA_ENV("DM_VK_OVERLAY", 1)` & `VKA_ENV("DM_VK_GPUPROF*")` preserved) — lock-free atomic `vka_ovl_pub_state` generation/non-empty guard (`vka_ovl_can_skip_lock`, `vka_ovl_has_content`, `vka_ovl_pub_bump`), `vka_ovl_copy_changed` early `memcmp` no-op exit and first-dirty-row tail copy (`ovl_prepare_upload`, `ovl_emit_quads`, `ovl_draw`), mutex-guarded 64-entry ring memo `vka_ovl_gpu_avg` in `vka_fps.c` (re-polling negative pending timestamps), and open-addressed hash tables `vka_gp_shape_ht` + `vka_gp_key3_ht` (`split_find_shape`, `split_accum_keys`) in `vka_gprof.c`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_ovl_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_ovl_fast.h), [`vk_overlay.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_overlay.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w40.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w40.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_OVL_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — `restrict`-qualified `totk_ovl_fill_u32`, `totk_ovl_raster_cell_fast` (`totk_ovl_cell_dx1` blank-row `skip_bg` + branchless `row[]` + `memcpy` for `scale=1,2`, `totk_ovl_cell_strided` fallback) + `totk_ovl_raster_col_fast` (`raster_cell_mode`, `raster_column_mode`), and POSIX `open`/`read`/`close` + exact-suffix parsers `totk_ovl_read_number_fast`, `totk_ovl_read_text_line`, `totk_ovl_parse_meminfo_buf`, and `totk_ovl_parse_status_rss_buf` (preserving stdio `read_number_slow` / fallback when `=0`).### Optimization 43 — AoI (`DM_FPS_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_PACE_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Guest Read Fast Path, Division-Reduced DRS Fitting, O(log N) Quantile Pacing, Sorting-Network Clock Fit & Bitmask Framegen Slots

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Present-Path Syscall/Scan Overhead & Scheduling Bookkeeping):**
  - **AoI — `hle_fpslock.c` / `hle_svc.c` / `drs_steps.h` (`dm/host/`):** Every present tick issued `plat_mem_read` syscalls in `hle_fpslock.c` even for immutable guest modules or mapped heap pages, computed duplicate `row_obj` lookups per lock row, used default seq-cst loads for live target/limit/hold60/decouple state, and executed floating-point divisions across candidate dynamic-resolution ratios in `drs_steps.h`.

  - **AoC:** [`vk_pace_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_pace_fast.h), [`predict_pace.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/predict_pace.h), [`display_clock.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/display_clock.h), [`fps_auto.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/fps_auto.h), [`framegen_adaptive_slots.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/framegen_adaptive_slots.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w41.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w41.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_PACE_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `vk_pace_fast.h` with `totk_pace_lb_i64` / `totk_pace_ub_i64` / `totk_pace_order_update_fast` (rank-preserving fast exit) and `totk_pace_count_gt_fast` for `predict_pace.h`; single-pass 8-division + `totk_display_sort8_u64` sorting network in `display_clock.h`; `totk_fps_auto_sample_ns` + `totk_fps_auto_evict_k` with `misses==0` early exit in `fps_auto.h`; and `totk_fgs_popcnt16` / `totk_fgs_ctz32` / `totk_fgs_ctz64` / `totk_fgs_first_free16` bitmask scans in `framegen_adaptive_slots.h` guarded by `popcnt16(pending_mask)==pending_count` and `occ_tracked` for white-box state safety.### Optimization 44 — AoI (`DM_TEX_LAYOUT_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_TEX_LAYOUT_FAST=1`): Table-Driven NVN/Vulkan Format & Block-Linear Layout Math, Mip-Walk Elision & Sparse/VPool Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Repeated Range-Scan Format Lookups, Division-Heavy Block Layout & Lock Contention):**
  - **AoI — `nvn_tex.c` / `nvn_fmt_mtl.c` / `vka_sparse.c` (`dm/gfx/`):** `fmt_of()` performed linear range scans across 37 intervals and ASTC `% 14` modulo; `blk()` and `adj_tile()` executed integer division and `while` shift loops per mip level; `nvn_tex_level_{offset,gobs,tiles}` and `nvn_tex_sparse_tiles` called `nvn_tex_layout()` (walking all mips) solely for `(tileH, tileD)`; `nvn_tex_level_of()` repeated nested per-level queries; and `vks_queue_locked()` acquired `g_ks` on every replay/upload submission even with no pending sparse binds.
  - **AoC — `nvn_tex.{c,h}` / `nvn_fmt_mtl.c` / `vk_fmt.c` / `totk_vpool.c` (`games/totk/`):** Paired `fmt_of()`, `blk()`, `adj_tile()`, and redundant `nvn_tex_layout()` pre-walks in `nvn_tex_level_{offset,tiles}`; switch-heavy `vk_fmt_class`, `vk_vertex_fmt`, and `vk_blend_factor` translation alongside `% 14` ASTC decoding; repeated `getpagesize()` / `% TOTK_VPOOL_PAGE` arithmetic and mutex-locked linear `g_vmap[512]` scans in `totk_vpool_backing()`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex_layout_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex_layout_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex.c), [`dm/gfx/tex/nvn_fmt_mtl.c`](../dm/gfx/tex/nvn_fmt_mtl.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_sparse.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_sparse.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_tex_layout_fast_w42.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_tex_layout_fast_w42.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_TEX_LAYOUT_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — 158-entry `k_nvn_fmt_lut[0x9D]` and inline ASTC rows in `nvn_fmt_mtl.c`; division-free magic-multiply `nvn_blk_fast`, CLZ `nvn_pick_tile_fast` / `nvn_adj_tile_fast`, power-of-two `tw` mask and `>> 14` `nvn_tbuf_rows`; O(1) `tile_hd_of` skipping unnecessary mip walks; single-pass `level_run_walk` + binary search in `nvn_tex_level_of`; and lock-free `nvn_sparse_try_idle` idle exit + stack batch arrays + `is_sorted` `qsort` skip + `g_tb_hint[32]` cursor in `vka_sparse.c`.
  - **AoC:** [`totk_tex_layout_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/totk_tex_layout_fast.h), [`nvn_tex.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex.h), [`nvn_tex.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_tex.c), [`nvn_fmt_mtl.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/tex/nvn_fmt_mtl.c), [`vk_fmt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_fmt.c), [`totk_vpool.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/totk_vpool.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w42.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w42.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_TEX_LAYOUT_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `totk_tex_layout_fast.h` providing 158-entry `k_totk_nvn_fmt_lut[0x9D]`, `totk_blk_fast`, `totk_pick_tile_fast`, `totk_adj_tile_fast`, and `totk_tile_hd_fast`; direct ASTC tables in `nvn_fmt_mtl.c` + `vk_fmt.c` alongside `k_fmt_class`/`k_vtx_fmt`/`k_blend_lut`; cached `host_page()`, `(TOTK_VPOOL_PAGE - 1u)` masking, and C11 `_Atomic`-field seqlock `totk_vmap_fast_t` (`512` entries, `1024`-slot hash index, `live_mask[8]` `ctzll` scan, `active_cnt==0` early exit) in `totk_vpool.c`.
### Optimization 45 — AoI (`DM_VK_QSCHED_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_SUBCAP_FAST=1`): Queue Scheduler Bitmasks/Rings/Gate Hashing & Lock-Free Counter, UBO Capture, and Slab Pool Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Linear Scheduler/Gate Table Scans, Re-Probes, & Capture/Counter Mutex Contention):**
  - **AoI — `vka_qsched.h` / `vka_mem.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `qs_whole` and `qs_commit_mark` linearly scanned `sc->mark[256]`; `qs_ev_rebuild` called `calloc`/`free` on every prune and `qs_ev_peak` rescanned the full open-addressed event table whenever `nev > evpeak`; `qs_poll`, `qs_parked`, `qs_park_min`, and `qs_force_all` scanned all `QS_NQ` queue slots even when unparked; `qs_gw_find` linearly scanned `gw[32]`; and `vka_mem.c` tested 4 KiB pages word-by-word and `gmap` ranges bit-by-bit.
  - **AoC — `nvn_vk.c` / `vk_draw.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** Every `vk_counter_value` / pair of `OP_RENDER_COND` checks acquired `g_cpend_mu` and scanned the ring buffer; `collect_ubos` walked command streams recursively (`calloc`/`memset` over `UBO_SEEN`) and grew heap `rng_t` buffers on every snapshot; `rng_sort` heap-allocated temporary scratch on calls `> 32`; `ucap_merge_ranges` repeated full `vk_resolve` lookups across consecutive sorted segments; and `ucap_build`/`ucap_free` issued 4–5 separate `malloc`/`free` calls per capture.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_qsched.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_mem.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_qsched_fast_w43.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_qsched_fast_w43.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_QSCHED_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — 256-bit sliding `mark_bits[4]` fence window (`ctz256`/`shr256`) + overflow promotion; inline `ev_inline[QS_NEV0]` table reuse with 256-slot fence-expiry ring (`vka_qs_ev_can_skip_peak` / `vka_qs_ev_advance_done` / `vka_qs_ev_note_signal` / `vka_qs_ev_resync`) + single-probe `qs_ev_find`; `uint16_t park_mask` `ctz` queue scan; 64-slot `gw_hash` gate lookup table; and 8-word unrolled `vka_page_nonzero_4k` + `vka_gmap_all_set`/`vka_gmap_set_range` with power-of-two granule shift.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_subcap_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_subcap_fast.h), [`vk_int.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_int.h), [`nvn_vk.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/nvn_vk.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w43.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w43.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_SUBCAP_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `vk_subcap_fast.h` providing seqlock + 512-bucket lock-free `totk_cpend_fast_t` (`vk_counter_value` & `vk_counter_pair`), TLS stack-iterative `collect_ubos` with epoch-tagged `totk_ubo_seen_tls` + stack `t_r_inl[4096]`, TLS-scratch `totk_radix_sort_rng`, monotonic `totk_span_win_t` elementary-segment window (`ucap_merge_ranges_fast`), and 4-slot atomic single-slab `totk_ucap_pool_t`.
### Optimization 46 — AoI (`DM_VK_DEV_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_GPUPROF_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Device Fence/Gate/Callback/Batch Fast Paths & GPU Profiler Pass-Key Folding, Format LUT, and Rank Selection

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex/Allocation Churn in Hot State Bookkeeping & Linear Classification/Sorting Overhead):**
  - **AoI — `vka_dev.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Hot fence queries, bumps, and max-advances serialized on `g_fence_mu`; signal paths issued unconditional `pthread_cond_broadcast` calls even when zero waiters were parked; `vka_after_free` callbacks took `after_mu` and `calloc`/`free` per entry; `vka_gate_ok`/`vka_gate_add` linearly scanned mutex-guarded `gban[64]`; `ub` batch checks linearly scanned small tables on misses; and median percentile selection sorted the full array (`O(n^2)`).
  - **AoC — `vk_gpuprof.c` / `vk_gpuprof_pipe.h` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `vk_gpuprof` hashed full pass keys byte-by-byte across zero-padded attachment tails and trailing fields and compared full structs with `memcmp`; `texel_bytes` walked a switch ladder on every attachment; top-64 report ranking called `qsort` across `GP_GROUPS`; `draw_shaders_hash` re-scanned draw lists in multiple passes; pipeline population computed power-of-two thresholds with a loop; and primitive estimation used division/branches per draw.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_dev.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_dev_fast_w44.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_dev_fast_w44.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_DEV_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — lock-free atomic `g_fence` (`vka_fence_load`/`vka_fence_bump`/`vka_fence_advance_max`), waiter-gated condvar broadcasts (`vka_waiter_enter`/`leave` + `vka_cond_broadcast_gated`), 256-slot lock-free `vka_after_pool_t`, 64-slot lock-free open-addressed `vka_gban_fast_t`, 16-slot Bloom+range `vka_ub_fast_t` (`vka_ub_is_batch_fast`), and iterative `O(n)` median-of-3 quickselect (`vka_select_kth_u64`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_gpuprof_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_gpuprof_fast.h), [`vk_gpuprof_pipe.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_gpuprof_pipe.h), [`vk_gpuprof.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_gpuprof.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w44.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w44.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_GPUPROF_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `vk_gpuprof_fast.h` providing bit-exact algebraic FNV-1a folding (`P^k` zero-attachment tails & precomputed `k_p20[0..10]` multipliers) in `vk_gp_key_hash_fast`, word-OR `vk_gp_key_eq_fast`, 128-byte LUT `vk_gp_texel_bytes_fast` (with `_Static_assert` on Khronos `VK_FORMAT_*` IDs), bottom-up heapsort/insertion `vk_gp_rank_sort_fast` + 64-entry min-heap `vk_gp_rank_top64_fast`, single-pass bounded `vk_gp_draw_shaders_hash_fast`, `__builtin_clz` `vk_gp_pipe_population_fast`, and shift/mul `vk_gp_pipe_primitives_fast`.
### Optimization 47 — AoI (`DM_VK_CTL_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_COMPUTE_FAST=1`): Lock-Free Env Override / Layer-Request Pair & Compute Queue / History / Translator Memo Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Mutex-Guarded Override/Queue Lookups, `O(N)` Linear Scans, Redundant Condvar Wakeups & Full-Table Allocations):**
  - **AoI — `vka_ctl.c` / `vka_layerreq.h` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** Every `str_get` env lookup took `g_emu` mutex and linearly scanned `g_ovr` even when empty; `kind_of` linearly scanned restart/live switch tables on every setting write; `vka_env_slow` called `strtod` even for `plain decimals; and `vka_lrq_*` formatted/parsed hex pairs via `snprintf`/`sscanf` (`line[96]` + ``uint64_t` pair I/O) and issued unconditional `pthread_cond_broadcast` when no receiver waiter slept.
  - **AoC — `vk_compute.c` / `vk_shader_xlate.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `enqueue`/`promote`/`worker` maintained singly-linked queues with `O(N)` tail walk on `promote` and unconditional `pthread_cond_broadcast` even with zero sleeping workers/waiters; `vk_compute_prepare` acquired `mu` even for already-seen pipeline keys; `vk_compute_pipeline` linearly `memcmp`-scanned up to `4095` `80`-byte history keys; `vk_shader_xlate_take_miss` took `xlate_mu` on empty ready queues; and `vk_shader_xlate_rejected` linearly `strcmp`-scanned rejected variant lists under `xlate_mu` (plus `test_vkshader_xlate.c` referenced stale `vk_seed_io_stats.inline_reads` instead of `demand_reads`).
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ctl_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ctl_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ctl.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_ctl.c), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_layerreq.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_layerreq.h), [`dm/host/tests/t_ctl_fast_w45.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_ctl_fast_w45.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_CTL_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — seqlock + open-addressed 128-slot `vka_ctl_ovr_fast_t`, 256-slot FNV-1a `vka_ctl_kind_fast`, `vka_ctl_parse_double` strict decimal fast path, branchless `vka_ctl_pair_line_put`/`parse` hex codecs, `pthread_cond_broadcast` gated on `_Atomic` waiter count in `vka_lrq_idle_broadcast`, and 64-bit `nctl+ncode` overflow guard.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_compute_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_compute_fast.h), [`vk_compute.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_compute.c), [`vk_shader_xlate.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_shader_xlate.c), [`test_vkshader_xlate.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vkshader_xlate.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w45.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w45.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_COMPUTE_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `vk_compute_fast.h` providing `O(1)` doubly-linked `totk_cp_queue_t`, `totk_cp_wake_enqueue`/`built` waiter/sleeper-gated wakeups, seqlock 2-way `totk_cp_prep_memo_t`, open-addressed 8192-slot FNV-1a `totk_cp_hist_t` index + shift-only eviction, seqlock 512-slot `totk_xlate_bad_fast_t`, `_Atomic uint32_t xlate_ready_n` fast-empty guard, and updated `test_vkshader_xlate.c` (`demand_reads`).
### Optimization 48 — AoI (`DM_VK_BIND_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_BIND_FAST=1`): Shift-Based Slot Decode, Sparse Driver-Bank Zero-Skipping, Template Support-Buffer Copy, Batched UBO-Array Handle Prefetch & Stage Binding / Descriptor Hash Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Per-Slot Division/Zero-Store Overhead on Sparse Driver Constant Banks, Repeated Support-Buffer Loops, Per-Element UBO Reads, and Unbundled Binding/Hash Loops):**
  - **AoI — `vka_bind.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `vka_c2_handle` used `% DMSH_C2_ROW` / `/ DMSH_C2_ROW` division; `vka_drv_fill` unconditionally `memcpy`'d empty SSBO/CS-UBO/c2 handle slots into zero-initialized `DMSH_DRV_SIZE` segments; `vka_support_fill` re-looped 73 `1.0f` render scales on every call; and `vka_walk` issued a separate `ubo_word` callback per element of `TEX_UBO`/`IMG_UBO`/`SAMP_UBO` arrays (including duplicate reads when a combined sampler shared the texture UBO).
  - **AoC — `vk_draw.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `driver_bank_fill` looped and stored zeros across all sparse SSBO, CS-UBO, texture, and sampler slots on top of `memset(..., 0, 0xA00)`; `support_fill` re-initialized `73` constant `render_scale` floats per call; `record_stage_fast_hit`/`record_bindings_same` re-read `S` stage fields on every `record_binding_of` iteration; and `hash_words` hashed 8 bytes sequentially rather than 2x-unrolled 16-byte blocks.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_bind_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_bind_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_bind.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_bind.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_bind_fast_w46.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_bind_fast_w46.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`dm/tools/lint_c_baseline.tsv`](../dm/tools/lint_c_baseline.tsv), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_BIND_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — shift/mask `vka_c2_handle_fast`, `vka_drv_c0_fast`/`vka_drv_c2_fast` with 8-slot OR zero-skipping (`vka_c2_span128_fast`) and `k_sample_blk` LUT, `pthread_once` `vka_support_fill_fast` template copy, batched `vka_ubo_handles_prefetch` (sharing `tw`/`sw` when `sreg == reg && soff == off`), and cleaned `walk_elem` `SBUF`/`UBO_PTR` bounds check removing all `5` `NullDereference` baseline findings (`395 -> 390`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_bind_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_bind_fast.h), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w46.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w46.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_BIND_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — ISO C11 `vk_bind_fast.h` providing `totk_drv_bank_fill_fast` (non-zero slot + 8-slot OR skip), `pthread_once` `totk_support_fill_fast`, hoisted `totk_bind_stage_ctx_t` (`totk_bind_fill_fast` / `totk_bind_eq_fast`), and 2x-unrolled `totk_hash_words_fast`.
### Optimization 49 — AoI (`DM_VK_FMT_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_CANVAS_FAST=1`): `O(1)` Texture Format LUT & Twin Maps, Lock-Free Format-Feature Cache, Stack-Buffered Parallel Upload Claim/Wake, Canvas UI/Transfer Shader Classification & Generic-Key Hash/Eq Fast Paths

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Switch-Based Format/Twin Decoding, Mutex/Bounded Format-Feature Caches, Upload Wake/Alloc Overhead, Linear Canvas Shader Scans & Bytewise Generic Hash Equality):**
  - **AoI — `vka_tex.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `fmt_ent_of` and `srgb_twin`/`raw_unorm_twin` evaluated large switches on every texture probe; `feats` acquired a global mutex and linearly scanned up to 128 cached formats on every lookup; and `upload_mt`/`up_run`/`up_parallel` allocated piece/part arrays on the heap, contended via CAS loops on `UP.next`, and unconditionally broadcast `UP.done_cv` under `UP.mu` even when no waiter slept.
  - **AoC — `vk_canvas.c`, `vk_res.c` & `nvn_hmap.h` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`, `third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/`):** `vk_canvas_ui_shader` linearly scanned 9–13 prefix strings with `strncmp`, `vk_canvas_transfer_shader` ran full `strncmp` without a fast 8-byte word pre-check, `slot` scanned all 16 entries without an MRU hint, `fmt_features` left extended formats (`>= 185`) uncached, and generic `nvn_hmap` keys (`>= 24` bytes) lacked 16B unrolled hashing and an 8-byte word prefix pre-filter before `memcmp`.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fmt_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_fmt_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_tex.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_fmt_fast_w47.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_fmt_fast_w47.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_FMT_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `g_vka_fmt_lut[0x9D]` `O(1)` format LUT, `vka_srgb_twin_fast`/`vka_raw_unorm_twin_fast` twin lookups (verified by `_Static_assert` against `VK_FORMAT_*` in `vka_tex.c`), lock-free atomic `vka_feats_cache_t` (`core[256]` + `ext[64]`), atomic `fetch_add` `vka_up_claim_fast`, waiter-gated `vka_up_finish_part_fast`, single-worker `pthread_cond_signal`, and `stack_pc[16]`/`stack_parts[64]` stack buffers in `upload_mt`.
  - **AoC:** [`vk_canvas_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_canvas_fast.h), [`vk_canvas.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_canvas.c), [`vk_res.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_res.c), [`nvn_hmap.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/third_party/d2r_gfx/gfx/nvn/nvn_hmap.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w47.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w47.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_CANVAS_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — pure C11 `vk_canvas_fast.h` (no `<pthread.h>`) providing `O(1)` 32-slot collision-free `totk_canvas_ui_shader_fast`, 8-byte word pre-checked `totk_canvas_transfer_shader_fast`, `totk_canvas_same_fast` + hint-cached `totk_canvas_slot_fast`, lock-free C11 `totk_fmt_feats_cache_t` (`core[256]` + atomic `ext[64]`), and self-contained 16B/24B unrolled `nvn_hm_hash` + 8-byte prefix `nvn_hm_eq_gen` in `nvn_hmap.h`.
### Optimization 50 — AoI (`DM_VK_PSO_KEY_FAST=1`) + AoC (`TOTK_VK_PSO_KEY_FAST=1`): `O(1)` PSO Integer/Enum LUTs, Zero-Write Last-Key Equality & Padding-Only Key Packing, Lock-Free Heavy-Set Filter, Bitmask Vertex/Attachment Walks & Precomputed GPL Dynamic-State Slices

- **Root Cause Across Both Titles (Bytewise PSO Key Equality & Redundant `memset`, Switch Enum/Format Decoding, Linear Heavy-Set/Dynamic-State Filtering & Full `0..31` Scans):**
  - **AoI — `vka_pso.c` (`dm/gfx/vk_android/`):** `vka_gfx_pipeline` unconditionally zeroed and packed `gkey_full` (`fk`) before comparing against `last`, while `km_key_eq` fell straight to `memcmp` on large struct keys; `pstate_fill` zeroed the entire `pstate` including `vb[33]/va[32]/vd[33]` even when `!k->vin_static`; `vkf_int_class` and `dyn_of`/`pstate` evaluated large switches/loops on every PSO lookup/GPL part; `heavy_has` linearly scanned up to 1,024 entries; and `fout_masks` scanned all 8 attachment slots bytewise.
  - **AoC — `vk_draw.c` & `vk_fmt.c` (`games/totk/android/gfx/`):** `pipe_get` compared `pipe_key` (`464` B) with tail-unprioritized `memcmp`; `pipe_write_mask`, `pipeline_blend_enable_for`, `pipe_key_fill`, and `pipe_create_kind` looped over full ranges instead of `__builtin_ctz` bitmasks; `pipe_create_kind` filtered 19 `VkDynamicState` entries per GPL library slice with nested branches; and `vk_blend_op`, `vk_stencil_op`, `vk_wrap`, `vk_swizzle_sel`, `vk_fmt_aspect`, `topo_class`, and `topology` evaluated switch ladders repeatedly during state/PSO translation.
- **Implementation:**
  - **AoI:** [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso_key_fast.h`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso_key_fast.h), [`dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c`](../dm/gfx/vk_android/vka_pso.c), [`dm/host/tests/t_pso_key_fast_w48.c`](../dm/host/tests/t_pso_key_fast_w48.c), [`dm/host/tests/Makefile`](../dm/host/tests/Makefile), [`docs/env_switches.tsv`](../docs/env_switches.tsv), [`docs/advanced_settings.tsv`](../docs/advanced_settings.tsv) (`DM_VK_PSO_KEY_FAST=1`, `0` disables) — `vka_gkey_same_last_fast` zero-write hit check + `vka_gkey_pack_fast` padding-only zeroing, `pstate` field reordering skipping 1,172 bytes of vertex-input `memset` when `!k->vin_static` (`pstate_fill_stages`/`pstate_fill`), first/last-word + 16B unrolled `vka_km_eq_fast`, `O(1)` `vka_vkf_int_class_fast` LUT, `__builtin_ctz` `vka_fout_masks_fast`, lock-free `vka_heavy_set_t` (`bloom` + 2048-slot table), and precomputed `vka_dyn_full_fast`/`vka_dyn_slice_fast` (all Vulkan constants checked by `_Static_assert` in `vka_pso.c`).
  - **AoC:** [`vk_pso_key_fast.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_pso_key_fast.h), [`vk_fmt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_fmt.c), [`vk_draw.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/vk_draw.c), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt_w48.h`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt_w48.h), [`test_vk_ktgl_opt.c`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/gfx/test_vk_ktgl_opt.c), [`Makefile`](https://github.com/P1R4N351/hwaoc-android/blob/main/games/totk/android/Makefile) (`TOTK_VK_PSO_KEY_FAST=1`, `0` disables; `0` new `#if.*GAME_` in `games/`) — pure C11 `vk_pso_key_fast.h` (no `<pthread.h>`) providing `O(1)` nibble-LUT `totk_pipe_write_mask_fast`, `totk_pipe_key_eq_fast` (`[0..16)` + `[128..144)` 64-bit word pre-check + SIMD `memcmp`), `totk_gpl_dyn_slice_fast` for GPL parts `0..3`, `__builtin_ctz` masks/loops in `pipe_key_fill` & `pipe_create_vin`, `totk_blend_en_fast`, and `O(1)` tables for `topo_class`, `topology`, `vk_blend_op`, `vk_stencil_op`, `vk_wrap`, `vk_swizzle_sel`, and `vk_fmt_aspect` (verified by `_Static_assert` on `pipe_key` offsets and Vulkan enums).
